/*!
 * @file Window.hpp
 * @brief Abstract platform-independent window interface.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Platform/Events/Event.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @struct WindowProps
   * @brief Configuration properties used to construct a native window.
   */
  struct WindowProps {
    std::string Title{"KanVerse Engine"};
    uint32_t    Width{1280};
    uint32_t    Height{720};
    bool        VSync{true};
    bool        Maximized{false};
  };
  /*!
   * @class Window
   * @brief Abstract base class defining the standard platform window contract.
   */
  class Window {
  public:
    using EventCallbackFn = std::function<void(Event&)>;
    virtual ~Window() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Dispatches OS window events and processes message queues.
     */
    virtual void OnUpdate() = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries current client width in logical points.
     * @return Horizontal extent.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetWidth() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries current client height in logical points.
     * @return Vertical extent.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetHeight() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Registers the event dispatch callback receiving routed OS events.
     * @param callback Function handler matching void(Event&).
     */
    virtual void SetEventCallback(const EventCallbackFn& callback) = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Enables or disables vertical synchronization.
     * @param enabled True to limit swap rate to display refresh.
     */
    virtual void SetVSync(bool enabled) = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries whether vertical synchronization is currently active.
     * @return True if VSync is enabled.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsVSync() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Retrieves the opaque native platform handle (e.g. NSWindow* on macOS).
     * @return Raw pointer to platform handle.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual void* GetNativeWindow() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Toggles or sets the window maximized/zoomed state.
     */
    virtual void Maximize() = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Checks if the window is currently zoomed/maximized.
     * @return True if maximized, false otherwise.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual bool IsMaximized() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Factory method constructing the concrete platform window implementation.
     * @param props Construction properties (title, dimensions, VSync).
     * @return Unique pointer owning the initialized Window instance.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<Window> Create(const WindowProps& props = WindowProps());
  };
} // namespace KanVerse

// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Buffers/BufferLayout.hpp
/*!
 * @file BufferLayout.hpp
 * @brief Memory layout reflection and descriptor types for GPU vertex attributes.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <cassert>
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @enum ShaderDataType
   * @brief Underlying data formats for vertex shader input attributes.
   */
  enum class ShaderDataType : uint8_t {
    None = 0,
    Float,
    Float2,
    Float3,
    Float4,
    Mat3,
    Mat4,
    Int,
    Int2,
    Int3,
    Int4,
    Bool
  };
  /*!
   * @brief Calculates size in bytes for a given shader attribute type adhering to Apple Silicon / SIMD 16-byte alignment.
   * @param type The shader attribute data format.
   * @return Size in bytes.
   */
  [[nodiscard]] constexpr uint32_t ShaderDataTypeSize(ShaderDataType type) noexcept {
    switch (type) {
      case ShaderDataType::Float:    return 4;
      case ShaderDataType::Float2:   return 8;
      case ShaderDataType::Float3:   return 16; // 16-byte aligned in SIMD/MSL float3
      case ShaderDataType::Float4:   return 16;
      case ShaderDataType::Mat3:     return 4 * 3 * 4;
      case ShaderDataType::Mat4:     return 4 * 4 * 4;
      case ShaderDataType::Int:      return 4;
      case ShaderDataType::Int2:     return 8;
      case ShaderDataType::Int3:     return 16;
      case ShaderDataType::Int4:     return 16;
      case ShaderDataType::Bool:     return 1;
      default: break;
    }
    return 0;
  }
  /*!
   * @struct BufferElement
   * @brief Descriptive metadata for a discrete element within an interleaved vertex buffer.
   */
  struct BufferElement {
    std::string    Name;
    ShaderDataType Type;
    uint32_t       Size;
    size_t         Offset{0};
    bool           Normalized{false};
    BufferElement() = default;
    BufferElement(ShaderDataType type, std::string name, bool normalized = false)
    : Name(std::move(name)),
    Type(type),
    Size(ShaderDataTypeSize(type)),
    Offset(0),
    Normalized(normalized) {}
    [[nodiscard]] constexpr uint32_t GetComponentCount() const noexcept {
      switch (Type) {
        case ShaderDataType::Float:   return 1;
        case ShaderDataType::Float2:  return 2;
        case ShaderDataType::Float3:  return 3;
        case ShaderDataType::Float4:  return 4;
        case ShaderDataType::Mat3:    return 9;
        case ShaderDataType::Mat4:    return 16;
        case ShaderDataType::Int:     return 1;
        case ShaderDataType::Int2:    return 2;
        case ShaderDataType::Int3:    return 3;
        case ShaderDataType::Int4:    return 4;
        case ShaderDataType::Bool:    return 1;
        default: break;
      }
      return 0;
    }
  };
  /*!
   * @class BufferLayout
   * @brief Encapsulates an ordered collection of BufferElements with automatic stride calculation.
   */
  class BufferLayout {
  public:
    BufferLayout() = default;
    BufferLayout(std::initializer_list<BufferElement> elements)
    : m_Elements(elements) {
      CalculateOffsetsAndStride();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t GetStride() const noexcept { return m_Stride; }
    [[nodiscard]] const std::vector<BufferElement>& GetElements() const noexcept { return m_Elements; }
    [[nodiscard]] auto begin() noexcept { return m_Elements.begin(); }
    [[nodiscard]] auto end() noexcept { return m_Elements.end(); }
    [[nodiscard]] auto begin() const noexcept { return m_Elements.begin(); }
    [[nodiscard]] auto end() const noexcept { return m_Elements.end(); }
  private:
    void CalculateOffsetsAndStride() {
      size_t offset = 0;
      m_Stride = 0;
      for (auto& element : m_Elements) {
        element.Offset = offset;
        offset += element.Size;
        m_Stride += element.Size;
      }
    }
    std::vector<BufferElement> m_Elements;
    uint32_t                   m_Stride{0};
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Buffers/IndexBuffer.hpp
/*!
 * @file IndexBuffer.hpp
 * @brief RHI index buffer interface wrapping Metal GPU memory.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
namespace MTL {
  class Buffer;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @enum IndexType
   * @brief Index element width (16-bit or 32-bit unsigned integers).
   */
  enum class IndexType : uint8_t {
    UInt16 = 0,
    UInt32
  };
  /*!
   * @class IndexBuffer
   * @brief RAII abstraction for GPU index/element buffers.
   */
  class IndexBuffer {
  public:
    virtual ~IndexBuffer() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries the element count in this index buffer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetCount() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries the index data type width (16-bit vs 32-bit).
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual IndexType GetIndexType() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the native underlying MTL::Buffer pointer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Buffer* GetNativeBuffer() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Factory creating an IndexBuffer populated with 16-bit indices.
     * @param indices Pointer to uint16_t array.
     * @param count Number of indices.
     * @return Unique pointer owning the IndexBuffer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<IndexBuffer> Create(const uint16_t* indices, uint32_t count);
    /*!
     * @brief Factory creating an IndexBuffer populated with 32-bit indices.
     * @param indices Pointer to uint32_t array.
     * @param count Number of indices.
     * @return Unique pointer owning the IndexBuffer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<IndexBuffer> Create(const uint32_t* indices, uint32_t count);
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Buffers/IndexBuffer.mm
/*!
 * @file IndexBuffer.mm
 * @brief Apple Metal implementation of IndexBuffer using metal-cpp.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Buffers/IndexBuffer.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
namespace KanVerse {
  class MetalIndexBuffer : public IndexBuffer {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Allocates unified memory for indices and binds RAII allocation tracker.
     * @param indices Raw pointer to index buffer array.
     * @param count Number of elements.
     * @param type Index element type (UInt16 or UInt32).
     * @param elementSize Byte width per element.
     */
    MetalIndexBuffer(const void* indices, uint32_t count, IndexType type, size_t elementSize)
    : m_Count(count), m_Type(type), m_MemoryGuard(count * elementSize) {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(indices != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Index source data pointer is null!");
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(count > 0, "[KanVerse] Index count must be greater than zero!");
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Active Metal device is null!");
      const size_t totalSize = count * elementSize;
      m_Buffer = device->newBuffer(indices, totalSize, MTL::ResourceStorageModeShared);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Buffer != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Failed to allocate MTL::Buffer for IndexBuffer!");
    }
    /*!
     * @brief Releases underlying native buffer; m_MemoryGuard automatically unwinds tracked bytes.
     */
    ~MetalIndexBuffer() override {
      if (m_Buffer) {
        m_Buffer->release();
        m_Buffer = nullptr;
      }
    }
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t GetCount() const noexcept override {
      return m_Count;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] IndexType GetIndexType() const noexcept override {
      return m_Type;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Buffer* GetNativeBuffer() const noexcept override {
      return m_Buffer;
    }
  private:
    MTL::Buffer*           m_Buffer{nullptr};                  /*!< Native Metal buffer pointer. */
    uint32_t               m_Count{0};                         /*!< Total number of indices. */
    IndexType              m_Type{IndexType::UInt16};          /*!< Element width identifier. */
    ScopedMemoryAllocation m_MemoryGuard{};                    /*!< Automated RAII memory tracking guard. */
  };
  std::unique_ptr<IndexBuffer> IndexBuffer::Create(const uint16_t* indices, uint32_t count) {
    return std::make_unique<MetalIndexBuffer>(indices, count, IndexType::UInt16, sizeof(uint16_t));
  }
  std::unique_ptr<IndexBuffer> IndexBuffer::Create(const uint32_t* indices, uint32_t count) {
    return std::make_unique<MetalIndexBuffer>(indices, count, IndexType::UInt32, sizeof(uint32_t));
  }
} // namespace KanVerse

/*!
 * @file VertexBuffer.hpp
 * @brief RHI vertex buffer interface wrapping Metal GPU memory.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Buffers/BufferLayout.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
namespace MTL {
  class Buffer;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @class VertexBuffer
   * @brief RAII abstraction for GPU vertex buffers allocated in unified memory.
   */
  class VertexBuffer {
  public:
    virtual ~VertexBuffer() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Binds buffer layout defining vertex attribute bindings.
     * @param layout Description of vertex memory strides and offsets.
     */
    virtual void SetLayout(const BufferLayout& layout) = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries the bound attribute layout.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual const BufferLayout& GetLayout() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the native underlying MTL::Buffer pointer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Buffer* GetNativeBuffer() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Size of the allocated buffer in bytes.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual size_t GetSize() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Factory method creating a VertexBuffer initialized with raw vertex memory.
     * @param vertices Pointer to source vertex data.
     * @param size Size in bytes to allocate.
     * @return Unique pointer owning the VertexBuffer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<VertexBuffer> Create(const void* vertices, size_t size);
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Buffers/VertexBuffer.mm
/*!
 * @file VertexBuffer.mm
 * @brief Apple Metal implementation of VertexBuffer using metal-cpp.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Buffers/VertexBuffer.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
namespace KanVerse {
  class MetalVertexBuffer : public VertexBuffer {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Allocates unified memory buffer and registers byte tracking via RAII guard.
     * @param vertices Pointer to source vertex data.
     * @param size Total buffer capacity in bytes.
     */
    MetalVertexBuffer(const void* vertices, size_t size)
    : m_Size(size), m_MemoryGuard(size) {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(size > 0, "[KanVerse] Cannot allocate a zero-sized VertexBuffer!");
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Active Metal device is null!");
      m_Buffer = device->newBuffer(vertices, size, MTL::ResourceStorageModeShared);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Buffer != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Failed to allocate MTL::Buffer for VertexBuffer!");
    }
    /*!
     * @brief Releases underlying native buffer; m_MemoryGuard automatically unwinds tracked bytes.
     */
    ~MetalVertexBuffer() override {
      if (m_Buffer) {
        m_Buffer->release();
        m_Buffer = nullptr;
      }
    }
    void SetLayout(const BufferLayout& layout) override {
      m_Layout = layout;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] const BufferLayout& GetLayout() const noexcept override {
      return m_Layout;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Buffer* GetNativeBuffer() const noexcept override {
      return m_Buffer;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] size_t GetSize() const noexcept override {
      return m_Size;
    }
  private:
    MTL::Buffer*           m_Buffer{nullptr};     /*!< Native Apple Metal buffer pointer. */
    size_t                 m_Size{0};             /*!< Total allocated size in bytes. */
    BufferLayout           m_Layout;              /*!< Stride and attribute reflection schema. */
    ScopedMemoryAllocation m_MemoryGuard{};       /*!< Automated RAII memory tracking guard. */
  };
  std::unique_ptr<VertexBuffer> VertexBuffer::Create(const void* vertices, size_t size) {
    return std::make_unique<MetalVertexBuffer>(vertices, size);
  }
} // namespace KanVerse

  class Camera {

    virtual ~Camera() = default;
    Camera() = default;

// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Camera/CameraController.hpp
/*!
 * @file CameraController.hpp
 * @brief Interactive orbit camera controller handling mouse dragging, zoom, and screen shake trauma.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Renderer/Camera/PerspectiveCamera.hpp"
#include "Core/Time/TimeStep.hpp"
#include "Platform/Events/Event.hpp"
#include "Platform/Events/WindowEvents.hpp"
#include "Platform/Events/MouseEvents.hpp"
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @class CameraController
   * @brief Orbit/Arcball camera controller responding to mouse interactions, focal tracking, and trauma impulses.
   */
  class CameraController {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Constructs an orbit camera controller with specified vertical FOV and aspect ratio.
     * @param fovDegrees Vertical field of view in degrees.
     * @param aspectRatio Viewport aspect ratio (width / height).
     */
    CameraController(float fovDegrees, float aspectRatio);
    /*!
     * @brief Frame-level update evaluating screen shake trauma decay and orientation.
     * @param ts Discrete delta time interval.
     */
    void OnUpdate(TimeStep ts);
    /*!
     * @brief Event routing callback processing mouse gestures and window resizes.
     * @param e Dispatched polymorphic event.
     */
    void OnEvent(Event& e);
    /*!
     * @brief Modifies camera orbit angles by applying mouse delta offsets.
     */
    void OnMousePan(float deltaX, float deltaY);
    /*!
     * @brief Zooms camera toward or away from focal target.
     */
    void OnMouseZoom(float deltaZoom);
    /*!
     * @brief Sets the radial distance from the focal tracking center.
     */
    void SetDistance(float distance) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] float GetDistance() const noexcept { return m_Distance; }
    /*!
     * @brief Sets the focal look-at center point in world space.
     * @param target Center point coordinates.
     */
    void SetTarget(const simd::float3& target);
    [[nodiscard]] const simd::float3& GetTarget() const noexcept { return m_FocalTarget; }
    /*!
     * @brief Queries current horizontal orbit yaw angle in radians.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] float GetYaw() const noexcept { return m_Yaw; }
    /*!
     * @brief Queries current vertical orbit pitch angle in radians.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] float GetPitch() const noexcept { return m_Pitch; }
    /*!
     * @brief Injects high-frequency camera trauma (clamped between 0.0 and 1.0).
     * @param amount Trauma value to accumulate.
     */
    void AddTrauma(float amount) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] PerspectiveCamera& GetCamera() noexcept { return m_Camera; }
    [[nodiscard]] const PerspectiveCamera& GetCamera() const noexcept { return m_Camera; }
  private:
    bool OnWindowResized(WindowResizeEvent& e);
    bool OnMouseMoved(MouseMovedEvent& e);
    bool OnMouseScrolled(MouseScrolledEvent& e);
    bool OnMouseButtonPressed(MouseButtonPressedEvent& e);
    bool OnMouseButtonReleased(MouseButtonReleasedEvent& e);
    /*!
     * @brief Recomputes eye position in Cartesian coordinates incorporating orbital angles and screen shake.
     */
    void UpdateCameraPosition();
    PerspectiveCamera m_Camera;                       ///< Underlying camera projection matrix manager.
    simd::float3      m_FocalTarget{0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};///< Target point tracked by the camera.
    float             m_Distance{32.0f};              ///< Radial orbit distance.
    float             m_Pitch{0.42f};                 ///< Vertical inclination angle in radians.
    float             m_Yaw{0.0f};                    ///< Horizontal azimuth angle in radians.
    // Screen Shake Trauma Metrics
    float             m_Trauma{0.0f};                 ///< Active trauma scalar [0, 1].
    float             m_ShakeTime{0.0f};              ///< High-frequency noise counter.
    float             m_LastMouseX{0.0f};             ///< Mouse baseline coordinate X.
    float             m_LastMouseY{0.0f};             ///< Mouse baseline coordinate Y.
    bool              m_IsDragging{false};            ///< Orbit drag state flag.
    bool              m_FirstClick{true};             ///< Baseline calibration guard.
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Camera/CameraController.mm
/*!
 * @file CameraController.mm
 * @brief Implementation of interactive orbit camera controller with non-linear screen shake trauma.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Camera/CameraController.hpp"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace KanVerse {
  CameraController::CameraController(float fovDegrees, float aspectRatio)
  : m_Camera(fovDegrees * (static_cast<float>(M_PI) / 180.0f), aspectRatio, 0.1f, 1000.0f),
  m_FocalTarget{0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f},
  m_Distance(32.0f),
  m_Pitch(0.42f),
  m_Yaw(0.0f),
  m_Trauma(0.0f),
  m_ShakeTime(0.0f),
  m_FirstClick(true) {
    UpdateCameraPosition();
  }
  void CameraController::OnUpdate(TimeStep ts) {
    const float dt = ts.GetSeconds();
    m_ShakeTime += dt * 45.0f; // High-frequency oscillation step for snappy micro-jitter
    // Decay trauma non-linearly over time
    if (m_Trauma > 0.0f) {
      m_Trauma = std::max(0.0f, m_Trauma - 1.6f * dt);
      UpdateCameraPosition();
    }
  }
  void CameraController::SetTarget(const simd::float3& target) {
    m_FocalTarget = target;
    UpdateCameraPosition();
  }
  void CameraController::AddTrauma(float amount) noexcept {
    // Accumulate trauma and clamp to normalized ceiling
    m_Trauma = std::clamp(m_Trauma + amount, 0.0f, 1.0f);
  }
  void CameraController::OnEvent(Event& e) {
    EventDispatcher dispatcher(e);
    dispatcher.Dispatch<WindowResizeEvent>([this](WindowResizeEvent& event) {
      return OnWindowResized(event);
    });
    dispatcher.Dispatch<MouseMovedEvent>([this](MouseMovedEvent& event) {
      return OnMouseMoved(event);
    });
    dispatcher.Dispatch<MouseScrolledEvent>([this](MouseScrolledEvent& event) {
      return OnMouseScrolled(event);
    });
    dispatcher.Dispatch<MouseButtonPressedEvent>([this](MouseButtonPressedEvent& event) {
      return OnMouseButtonPressed(event);
    });
    dispatcher.Dispatch<MouseButtonReleasedEvent>([this](MouseButtonReleasedEvent& event) {
      return OnMouseButtonReleased(event);
    });
  }
  bool CameraController::OnWindowResized(WindowResizeEvent& e) {
    m_Camera.SetViewportSize(e.GetWidth(), e.GetHeight());
    return false;
  }
  bool CameraController::OnMouseButtonPressed(MouseButtonPressedEvent& e) {
    if (e.GetMouseButton() == 0 || e.GetMouseButton() == 1) {
      m_IsDragging = true;
      m_FirstClick = true; // Prevent delta spike from stale coordinates
    }
    return false;
  }
  bool CameraController::OnMouseButtonReleased(MouseButtonReleasedEvent& e) {
    if (e.GetMouseButton() == 0 || e.GetMouseButton() == 1) {
      m_IsDragging = false;
      m_FirstClick = true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  bool CameraController::OnMouseMoved(MouseMovedEvent& e) {
    if (!m_IsDragging) {
      m_LastMouseX = e.GetX();
      m_LastMouseY = e.GetY();
      return false;
    }
    if (m_FirstClick) {
      m_LastMouseX = e.GetX();
      m_LastMouseY = e.GetY();
      m_FirstClick = false;
      return false;
    }
    const float deltaX = e.GetX() - m_LastMouseX;
    const float deltaY = e.GetY() - m_LastMouseY;
    m_LastMouseX = e.GetX();
    m_LastMouseY = e.GetY();
    OnMousePan(deltaX, deltaY);
    return false;
  }
  bool CameraController::OnMouseScrolled(MouseScrolledEvent& e) {
    const float clampedDelta = std::clamp(e.GetYOffset(), -20.0f, 20.0f);
    OnMouseZoom(clampedDelta * 0.05f);
    return false;
  }
  void CameraController::OnMousePan(float deltaX, float deltaY) {
    constexpr float sensitivity = 0.005f;
    m_Yaw   -= deltaX * sensitivity;
    m_Pitch += deltaY * sensitivity;
    // Avoid gimbal lock near polar poles
    constexpr float maxPitch = 1.50f;
    constexpr float minPitch = -1.50f;
    m_Pitch = std::clamp(m_Pitch, minPitch, maxPitch);
    UpdateCameraPosition();
  }
  void CameraController::OnMouseZoom(float deltaZoom) {
    const float zoomSpeed = std::max(m_Distance * 0.05f, 0.35f);
    m_Distance -= deltaZoom * zoomSpeed;
    m_Distance = std::clamp(m_Distance, 2.0f, 75.0f);
    UpdateCameraPosition();
  }
  void CameraController::SetDistance(float distance) noexcept {
    m_Distance = std::clamp(distance, 2.0f, 75.0f);
    UpdateCameraPosition();
  }
  void CameraController::UpdateCameraPosition() {
    const float cosPitch = std::cos(m_Pitch);
    const float sinPitch = std::sin(m_Pitch);
    const float cosYaw   = std::cos(m_Yaw);
    const float sinYaw   = std::sin(m_Yaw);
    // Base orbital position in Cartesian space
    simd::float3 eyePos = m_FocalTarget + simd_make_float3(
                                                           m_Distance * cosPitch * sinYaw,
                                                           m_Distance * sinPitch,
                                                           m_Distance * cosPitch * cosYaw
                                                           );
    // Apply quadratic trauma shake for organic, impactful screen rumble
    if (m_Trauma > 1e-4f) {
      const float shakeFactor = m_Trauma * m_Trauma * 0.28f;
      const float offsetX = std::sin(m_ShakeTime * 1.1f) * shakeFactor;
      const float offsetY = std::cos(m_ShakeTime * 1.4f) * shakeFactor;
      const float offsetZ = std::sin(m_ShakeTime * 1.7f) * shakeFactor;
      eyePos += simd_make_float3(offsetX, offsetY, offsetZ);
      m_Camera.SetTarget(m_FocalTarget + simd_make_float3(offsetX * 0.5f, offsetY * 0.5f, offsetZ * 0.5f));
    } else {
      m_Camera.SetTarget(m_FocalTarget);
    }
    m_Camera.SetPosition(eyePos);
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Camera/PerspectiveCamera.cpp
/*!
 * @file PerspectiveCamera.cpp
 * @brief Implementation of the 3D perspective camera matrix calculations.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Camera/PerspectiveCamera.hpp"
namespace KanVerse {
  PerspectiveCamera::PerspectiveCamera(float fovRadians, float aspectRatio, float nearClip, float farClip)
  : m_FovRadians(fovRadians),
  m_AspectRatio(aspectRatio),
  m_NearClip(nearClip),
  m_FarClip(farClip) {
    RecalculateProjectionMatrix();
    RecalculateViewMatrix();
  }
  void PerspectiveCamera::SetViewportSize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height) {
    if (width == 0 || height == 0) return;
    m_AspectRatio = static_cast<float>(width) / static_cast<float>(height);
    RecalculateProjectionMatrix();
  }
  void PerspectiveCamera::SetPosition(const Math::Vec3& position) {
    m_Position = position;
    RecalculateViewMatrix();
  }
  void PerspectiveCamera::SetTarget(const Math::Vec3& target) {
    m_Target = target;
    RecalculateViewMatrix();
  }
  void PerspectiveCamera::RecalculateProjectionMatrix() {
    m_Projection = Math::Perspective(m_FovRadians, m_AspectRatio, m_NearClip, m_FarClip);
    m_ViewProjectionMatrix = Math::Multiply(m_Projection, m_ViewMatrix);
  }
  void PerspectiveCamera::RecalculateViewMatrix() {
    m_ViewMatrix = Math::LookAt(m_Position, m_Target, m_Up);
    m_ViewProjectionMatrix = Math::Multiply(m_Projection, m_ViewMatrix);
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Camera/PerspectiveCamera.hpp
/*!
 * @file PerspectiveCamera.hpp
 * @brief 3D perspective camera computing view and view-projection matrices.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Renderer/Camera/Camera.hpp"
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @class PerspectiveCamera
   * @brief 3D perspective camera supporting positional movement, pitch, yaw, and target tracking.
   */
  class PerspectiveCamera : public Camera {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Constructs a perspective camera.
     * @param fovRadians Vertical field of view in radians.
     * @param aspectRatio Viewport width over height.
     * @param nearClip Near clipping distance.
     * @param farClip Far clipping distance.
     */
    PerspectiveCamera(float fovRadians, float aspectRatio, float nearClip = 0.1f, float farClip = 1000.0f);
    /*!
     * @brief Updates projection matrix when viewport dimensions mutate.
     * @param width Viewport width in logical points.
     * @param height Viewport height in logical points.
     */
    void SetViewportSize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height);
    /*!
     * @brief Sets world position and recalculates view and view-projection transforms.
     * @param position New camera coordinates in world space.
     */
    void SetPosition(const Math::Vec3& position);
    /*!
     * @brief Sets focal look-at target and recalculates view matrices.
     * @param target Center point the camera faces.
     */
    void SetTarget(const Math::Vec3& target);
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Vec3& GetPosition() const noexcept { return m_Position; }
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Vec3& GetTarget() const noexcept { return m_Target; }
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Mat4& GetViewMatrix() const noexcept { return m_ViewMatrix; }
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Mat4& GetViewProjectionMatrix() const noexcept { return m_ViewProjectionMatrix; }
  private:
    void RecalculateViewMatrix();
    void RecalculateProjectionMatrix();
    float      m_FovRadians;
    float      m_AspectRatio;
    float      m_NearClip;
    float      m_FarClip;
    Math::Vec3 m_Position{0.0f, 0.0f, 3.0f};
    Math::Vec3 m_Target{0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};
    Math::Vec3 m_Up{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};
    Math::Mat4 m_ViewMatrix{Math::Identity()};
    Math::Mat4 m_ViewProjectionMatrix{Math::Identity()};
  };
} // namespace KanVerse

/*!
 * @file RenderContext.hpp
 * @brief RAII encapsulation of Apple Metal device and command queue hardware handles.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
namespace MTL {
  class Device;
  class CommandQueue;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @class RenderContext
   * @brief Manages the primary Metal hardware context, device querying, and command queue.
   */
  class RenderContext {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Queries the system default Metal device and initializes the primary command queue.
     */
    RenderContext();
    /*!
     * @brief Releases the command queue and device hardware handles.
     */
    ~RenderContext();
    RenderContext(const RenderContext&) = delete;
    RenderContext& operator=(const RenderContext&) = delete;
    RenderContext(RenderContext&&) noexcept;
    RenderContext& operator=(RenderContext&&) noexcept;
    /*!
     * @brief Retrieves the active Metal system device pointer.
     * @return Raw pointer to MTL::Device.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Device* GetDevice() const noexcept { return m_Device; }
    /*!
     * @brief Retrieves the active primary command queue pointer.
     * @return Raw pointer to MTL::CommandQueue.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::CommandQueue* GetCommandQueue() const noexcept { return m_CommandQueue; }
    /*!
     * @brief Factory constructor generating an initialized RenderContext instance.
     * @return Unique pointer owning the initialized RenderContext.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::unique_ptr<RenderContext> Create();
  private:
    MTL::Device*       m_Device{nullptr};
    MTL::CommandQueue* m_CommandQueue{nullptr};
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Context/RenderContext.mm
/*!
 * @file RenderContext.mm
 * @brief Implementation of the Metal render context using metal-cpp and Foundation.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Log.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Context/RenderContext.hpp"
// metal-cpp headers in canonical order
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
#include <QuartzCore/QuartzCore.hpp>
// Apple Native Frameworks
#import <Metal/Metal.h>
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
#include <cassert>
namespace KanVerse {
  std::unique_ptr<RenderContext> RenderContext::Create() {
    return std::make_unique<RenderContext>();
  }
  RenderContext::RenderContext() {
    // Retrieve system default device via native runtime and bridge to metal-cpp wrapper
    id<MTLDevice> sysDevice = MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice();
    if (!sysDevice) {
      KV_CORE_ERROR("Metal is not supported on this host.");
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(false, "Metal is not supported on this host.");
      return;
    }
    m_Device = (__bridge MTL::Device*)sysDevice;
    m_CommandQueue = m_Device->newCommandQueue();
    if (!m_CommandQueue) {
      KV_CORE_ERROR("Failed to create primary MTL::CommandQueue.");
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(false, "Failed to create primary MTL::CommandQueue.");
      return;
    }
    KV_CORE_INFO("RenderContext initialized successfully on device: %s",
                 m_Device->name()->utf8String());
  }
  RenderContext::~RenderContext() {
    if (m_CommandQueue) {
      m_CommandQueue->release();
      m_CommandQueue = nullptr;
    }
    if (m_Device) {
      m_Device->release();
      m_Device = nullptr;
    }
  }
  RenderContext::RenderContext(RenderContext&& other) noexcept
  : m_Device(other.m_Device), m_CommandQueue(other.m_CommandQueue) {
    other.m_Device = nullptr;
    other.m_CommandQueue = nullptr;
  }
  RenderContext& RenderContext::operator=(RenderContext&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      if (m_CommandQueue) m_CommandQueue->release();
      if (m_Device) m_Device->release();
      m_Device = other.m_Device;
      m_CommandQueue = other.m_CommandQueue;
      other.m_Device = nullptr;
      other.m_CommandQueue = nullptr;
    }
    return *this;
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Framebuffer/Framebuffer.hpp
/*!
 * @file Framebuffer.hpp
 * @brief Offscreen render target abstraction managing color and depth textures.
 * @details Encapsulates GPU color attachments, depth-stencil textures, and preconfigured
 *          Metal render pass descriptors conforming to HDR floating-point targets.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
namespace MTL {
  class Texture;
  class RenderPassDescriptor;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @enum FramebufferPixelFormat
   * @brief Supported color attachment pixel formats.
   */
  enum class FramebufferPixelFormat : uint8_t {
    RGBA8 = 0,    ///< Standard 8-bit SDR format.
    RGBA16Float   ///< Half-precision 16-bit float format for HDR radiance.
  };
  /*!
   * @struct FramebufferSpecification
   * @brief Configuration properties used to allocate offscreen GPU framebuffer targets.
   */
  struct FramebufferSpecification {
    uint32_t               Width{1280};                                    ///< Viewport pixel width.
    uint32_t               Height{720};                                   ///< Viewport pixel height.
    uint32_t               Samples{1};                                    ///< Multi-sample anti-aliasing count.
    FramebufferPixelFormat Format{FramebufferPixelFormat::RGBA16Float};   ///< Defaults to 16-bit half float HDR target.
    bool                   SwapChainTarget{false};                        ///< Direct swapchain rendering flag.
    bool                   DepthOnly{false};                              ///< True if allocating a shadow depth map.
    float                  ClearColor[4]{0.06f, 0.07f, 0.09f, 1.0f};      ///< Default background clear color (RGBA).
  };
  /*!
   * @class Framebuffer
   * @brief High-level hardware render target container managing color and depth-stencil textures.
   */
  class Framebuffer {
  public:
    virtual ~Framebuffer() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Resizes backing GPU textures and reallocates attachment allocations.
     * @param width New pixel width.
     * @param height New pixel height.
     */
    virtual void Resize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height) = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries configuration specification.
     * @return Const reference to specification.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual const FramebufferSpecification& GetSpecification() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the color attachment texture.
     * @param index Attachment slot index (defaults to primary target 0).
     * @return Raw pointer to MTL::Texture (nullptr if DepthOnly is true).
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Texture* GetColorAttachment(uint32_t index = 0) const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the depth-stencil attachment texture.
     * @return Raw pointer to MTL::Texture.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Texture* GetDepthAttachment() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the preconfigured Metal render pass descriptor.
     * @return Raw pointer to MTL::RenderPassDescriptor.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::RenderPassDescriptor* GetRenderPassDescriptor() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Factory creating an offscreen Framebuffer instance.
     * @param spec Specification configuration.
     * @return Shared pointer owning the Framebuffer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> Create(const FramebufferSpecification& spec);
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Framebuffer/Framebuffer.mm
/*!
 * @file Framebuffer.mm
 * @brief Apple Metal implementation of offscreen Framebuffer supporting HDR textures.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Framebuffer/Framebuffer.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
namespace KanVerse {
  class MetalFramebuffer : public Framebuffer {
  public:
    explicit MetalFramebuffer(const FramebufferSpecification& spec)
    : m_Spec(spec) {
      Invalidate();
    }
    ~MetalFramebuffer() override {
      ReleaseResources();
    }
    void Resize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height) override {
      if (width == 0 || height == 0 || (m_Spec.Width == width && m_Spec.Height == height)) {
        return;
      }
      m_Spec.Width = width;
      m_Spec.Height = height;
      Invalidate();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] const FramebufferSpecification& GetSpecification() const noexcept override {
      return m_Spec;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Texture* GetColorAttachment(uint32_t index) const noexcept override {
      if (m_Spec.DepthOnly) {
        return nullptr;
      }
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(index == 0, "[KanVerse] Multiple color attachments currently unsupported!");
      return m_ColorTexture;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Texture* GetDepthAttachment() const noexcept override {
      return m_DepthTexture;
    }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::RenderPassDescriptor* GetRenderPassDescriptor() const noexcept override {
      return m_PassDescriptor;
    }
  private:
    void ReleaseResources() {
      if (m_PassDescriptor) {
        m_PassDescriptor->release();
        m_PassDescriptor = nullptr;
      }
      if (m_DepthTexture) {
        m_DepthTexture->release();
        m_DepthTexture = nullptr;
      }
      if (m_ColorTexture) {
        m_ColorTexture->release();
        m_ColorTexture = nullptr;
      }
    }
    void Invalidate() {
      ReleaseResources();
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Active Metal device is null!");
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Spec.Width > 0 && m_Spec.Height > 0, "[KanVerse] Viewport dimensions must be non-zero!");
      size_t totalTextureBytes = 0;
      // 1. Allocate Color Attachment (RGBA16Float for HDR or BGRA8 for SDR)
      if (!m_Spec.DepthOnly) {
        const MTL::PixelFormat colorFormat = (m_Spec.Format == FramebufferPixelFormat::RGBA16Float)
        ? MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float
        : MTL::PixelFormatBGRA8Unorm_sRGB;
        const size_t bytesPerPixel = (m_Spec.Format == FramebufferPixelFormat::RGBA16Float) ? 8 : 4;
        auto colorDesc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::texture2DDescriptor(
                                                                     colorFormat,
                                                                     m_Spec.Width,
                                                                     m_Spec.Height,
                                                                     false
                                                                     );
        colorDesc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageRenderTarget | MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
        colorDesc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModePrivate);
        m_ColorTexture = device->newTexture(colorDesc);
        colorDesc->release();
        KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_ColorTexture != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Failed to allocate Framebuffer Color Texture!");
        totalTextureBytes += static_cast<size_t>(m_Spec.Width) * m_Spec.Height * bytesPerPixel;
      }
      // 2. Allocate Depth Texture (Depth32Float -> 4 bytes per pixel)
      auto depthDesc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::texture2DDescriptor(
                                                                   MTL::PixelFormatDepth32Float,
                                                                   m_Spec.Width,
                                                                   m_Spec.Height,
                                                                   false
                                                                   );
      depthDesc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageRenderTarget | MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
      depthDesc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModePrivate);
      m_DepthTexture = device->newTexture(depthDesc);
      depthDesc->release();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_DepthTexture != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Failed to allocate Framebuffer Depth Texture!");
      totalTextureBytes += static_cast<size_t>(m_Spec.Width) * m_Spec.Height * 4;
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(totalTextureBytes);
      // 3. Build Reusable MTL::RenderPassDescriptor
      m_PassDescriptor = MTL::RenderPassDescriptor::renderPassDescriptor();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_PassDescriptor != nullptr, "[KanVerse] Failed to create MTL::RenderPassDescriptor!");
      if (!m_Spec.DepthOnly) {
        auto colorAttachment = m_PassDescriptor->colorAttachments()->object(0);
        colorAttachment->setTexture(m_ColorTexture);
        colorAttachment->setLoadAction(MTL::LoadActionClear);
        colorAttachment->setStoreAction(MTL::StoreActionStore);
        colorAttachment->setClearColor(MTL::ClearColor::Make(
                                                             m_Spec.ClearColor[0],
                                                             m_Spec.ClearColor[1],
                                                             m_Spec.ClearColor[2],
                                                             m_Spec.ClearColor[3]
                                                             ));
      }
      auto depthAttachment = m_PassDescriptor->depthAttachment();
      depthAttachment->setTexture(m_DepthTexture);
      depthAttachment->setLoadAction(MTL::LoadActionClear);
      depthAttachment->setStoreAction(MTL::StoreActionStore);
      depthAttachment->setClearDepth(1.0);
    }
    FramebufferSpecification   m_Spec;
    MTL::Texture*              m_ColorTexture{nullptr};
    MTL::Texture*              m_DepthTexture{nullptr};
    MTL::RenderPassDescriptor* m_PassDescriptor{nullptr};
    ScopedMemoryAllocation     m_MemoryGuard{};
  };
  std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> Framebuffer::Create(const FramebufferSpecification& spec) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalFramebuffer>(spec);
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Geometry/Mesh.cpp
/*!
 * @file Mesh.cpp
 * @brief Implementation of high-level Mesh subsystem.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Geometry/Mesh.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
namespace KanVerse {
  Mesh::Mesh(const Vertex* vertices,
             size_t vertexCount,
             const uint16_t* indices,
             uint32_t indexCount,
             const BufferLayout& layout) {
    m_VertexBuffer = VertexBuffer::Create(vertices, vertexCount * sizeof(Vertex));
    m_VertexBuffer->SetLayout(layout);
    m_IndexBuffer = IndexBuffer::Create(indices, indexCount);
    Submesh rootSubmesh;
    rootSubmesh.NodeName = "RootSubmesh";
    rootSubmesh.BaseVertex = 0;
    rootSubmesh.BaseIndex = 0;
    rootSubmesh.IndexCount = indexCount;
    rootSubmesh.MaterialIndex = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < vertexCount; ++i) {
      rootSubmesh.BoundingBox.Encapsulate(vertices[i].position);
    }
    m_Submeshes.push_back(rootSubmesh);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Mesh> Mesh::CreateChamferBox(float size,
                                               float chamfer,
                                               const BufferLayout& layout) {
    MeshData meshData = ChamferBoxGenerator::Generate(size, chamfer);
    return std::make_shared<Mesh>(
                                  meshData.vertices.data(),
                                  meshData.vertices.size(),
                                  meshData.indices.data(),
                                  static_cast<uint32_t>(meshData.indices.size()),
                                  layout
                                  );
  }
} // namespace KanVerse

    Mesh(const Vertex* vertices,

         uint32_t indexCount,

    /*!

    /*!
     * @brief Accesses the submesh partition collection.

    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Mesh> CreateChamferBox(float size,
                                                                float chamfer,
                                                                const BufferLayout& layout);
  private:
    std::shared_ptr<VertexBuffer> m_VertexBuffer;
    std::shared_ptr<IndexBuffer>  m_IndexBuffer;
    std::vector<Submesh>          m_Submeshes;
  };

// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Geometry/SubMesh.hpp
/*!
 * @file Submesh.hpp
 * @brief Discrete submesh draw-call descriptors and local bounds definition.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Math/Math.hpp"
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @struct AABB
   * @brief Axis-aligned bounding box defined by minimum and maximum extents in 3D space.
   */
  struct AABB {
    Math::Vec3 Min{ 1e30f,  1e30f,  1e30f};
    Math::Vec3 Max{-1e30f, -1e30f, -1e30f};
    /*!
     * @brief Expands bounding extents to encapsulate an arbitrary point.
     * @param point 3D coordinates.
     */
    void Encapsulate(const Math::Vec3& point) {
      Min = Math::MakeVec3(
                           std::min(Min.x, point.x),
                           std::min(Min.y, point.y),
                           std::min(Min.z, point.z)
                           );
      Max = Math::MakeVec3(
                           std::max(Max.x, point.x),
                           std::max(Max.y, point.y),
                           std::max(Max.z, point.z)
                           );
    }
  };
  /*!
   * @struct Submesh
   * @brief Partitioned primitive geometry metadata mapping to a dedicated draw-call.
   */
  struct Submesh {
    std::string NodeName{"Submesh"};      ///< Semantic diagnostic identifier.
    uint32_t    BaseVertex{0};             ///< Starting vertex offset in primary vertex stream.
    uint32_t    BaseIndex{0};              ///< Starting byte offset in primary index stream.
    uint32_t    IndexCount{0};             ///< Number of primitive indices.
    uint32_t    MaterialIndex{0};          ///< Target surface material index.
    Math::Mat4  LocalTransform{Math::Identity()}; ///< Hierarchical transformation.
    AABB        BoundingBox;               ///< Local object space extents.
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Lighting/DirectionalLight.hpp
/*!
 * @file DirectionalLight.hpp
 * @brief Directional sun/moon light source with orthographic shadow-map projection matrix generation.
 * @copyright KanVerse Engine
 */
#pragma once
#include "Renderer/Lighting/Light.hpp"
#include "Core/Math/Math.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @brief Represents an infinite directional light source (Sun/Moon) and its orthographic shadow frustum.
   */
  class DirectionalLight : public Light {
  public:
    /*!
     * @brief Constructs a DirectionalLight with direction, color, and intensity.
     * @param direction Normalized or unnormalized light travel direction vector.
     * @param color Linear RGB color of the light.
     * @param intensity Luminous intensity multiplier.
     */
    explicit DirectionalLight(const Math::Vec3& direction = Math::MakeVec3(-0.6f, -1.0f, -0.4f),
                              const Math::Vec3& color = Math::MakeVec3(1.0f, 0.95f, 0.88f),
                              float intensity = 1.2f)
    : Light(color, intensity),
    m_Direction(simd_normalize(direction)),
    m_OrthoExtent(75.0f) {
      RecalculateMatrices();
    }
    /*!
     * @brief Updates the light direction and recalculates the orthographic light-space matrix.
     * @param direction New light direction vector.
     */
    void SetDirection(const Math::Vec3& direction) noexcept {
      m_Direction = simd_normalize(direction);
      RecalculateMatrices();
    }
    /*!
     * @brief Sets the orthographic half-extent for the shadow map frustum.
     * @param extent Half-width and half-height of the orthographic shadow box.
     */
    void SetOrthoExtent(float extent) noexcept {
      m_OrthoExtent = std::max(extent, 75.0f);
      RecalculateMatrices();
    }
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Vec3& GetDirection() const noexcept { return m_Direction; }
    [[nodiscard]] float GetOrthoExtent() const noexcept { return m_OrthoExtent; }
    [[nodiscard]] const Math::Mat4& GetViewProjectionMatrix() const noexcept { return m_ViewProjectionMatrix; }
    [[nodiscard]] LightType GetType() const noexcept override { return LightType::Directional; }
  private:
    /*!
     * @brief Recomputes the orthographic View-Projection matrix covering the entire level ground plane.
     */
    void RecalculateMatrices() noexcept {
      const Math::Vec3 lightPos = -m_Direction * 90.0f;
      const Math::Vec3 target   = Math::MakeVec3(0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f);
      const Math::Vec3 up       = Math::MakeVec3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
      const Math::Mat4 lightView = Math::LookAt(lightPos, target, up);
      const Math::Mat4 lightProj = Math::Ortho(
                                               -m_OrthoExtent, m_OrthoExtent,
                                               -m_OrthoExtent, m_OrthoExtent,
                                               1.0f,           220.0f
                                               );
      m_ViewProjectionMatrix = Math::Multiply(lightProj, lightView);
    }
    Math::Vec3 m_Direction;                              ///< Normalized light direction vector.
    float      m_OrthoExtent;                            ///< Half-extent of the orthographic shadow volume.
    Math::Mat4 m_ViewProjectionMatrix{Math::Identity()}; ///< Combined light-space projection * view matrix.
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Lighting/EnvironmentLighting.hpp
/*!
 * @file EnvironmentLighting.hpp
 * @brief Ambient environmental illumination parameters.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Math/Math.hpp"
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @struct EnvironmentLighting
   * @brief Environmental ambient radiant parameters.
   */
  struct EnvironmentLighting {
    Math::Vec3 AmbientColor{0.18f, 0.20f, 0.24f}; ///< Background ambient tint.
    float      AmbientIntensity{1.0f};             ///< Global ambient scalar.
    /*!
     * @brief Computes effective scaled ambient color vector.
     * @return 4D vector formatted for GPU uniform blocks.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] Math::Vec4 GetEffectiveAmbient() const noexcept {
      return Math::MakeVec4(
                            AmbientColor.x * AmbientIntensity,
                            AmbientColor.y * AmbientIntensity,
                            AmbientColor.z * AmbientIntensity,
                            1.0f
                            );
    }
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
