/*!
 * @file Renderer.hpp
 * @brief High-level Metal rendering orchestrator with instancing, shadows, transparency, IBL, point lights, and bloom.
 * @copyright KanVerse Engine
 */
#pragma once
#include "Core/Math/Math.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Buffers/IndexBuffer.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Buffers/VertexBuffer.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Framebuffer/Framebuffer.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Geometry/Mesh.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Lighting/DirectionalLight.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Lighting/EnvironmentLighting.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Materials/Material.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Pipeline/FrameSync.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Pipeline/Pipeline.hpp"
#include "Renderer/PostProcess/BloomPass.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Textures/TextureCube.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Camera/PerspectiveCamera.hpp"
#include <span>
#include <vector>
namespace MTL {
  class Buffer;
  class CommandBuffer;
  class RenderCommandEncoder;
  class SamplerState;
  class RenderPipelineState;
  class DepthStencilState;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @brief Environment lighting source mode.
   */
  enum class EnvironmentMode : uint8_t {
    CrispHDR = 0, ///< High-dynamic-range equirectangular skybox.
    Irradiance,   ///< Convolved diffuse irradiance skybox.
    Procedural    ///< Analytic gradient procedural skybox.
  };
  /*!
   * @brief Per-instance transformation matrices uploaded to vertex buffer(3).
   */
  struct alignas(16) InstanceData {
    Math::Mat4 ModelMatrix;  ///< Local-to-world transformation matrix.
    Math::Mat4 NormalMatrix; ///< Inverse-transpose matrix for normal transformation.
  };
  /*!
   * @brief GPU-aligned point light structure carrying world position, color, intensity, and physical attenuation.
   */
  struct alignas(16) GPUPointLight {
    Math::Vec4 PositionAndRadius;  ///< xyz = World Position, w = Cutoff Radius.
    Math::Vec4 ColorAndIntensity;  ///< rgb = Linear Color, a = Luminous Intensity.
    Math::Vec4 Attenuation;        ///< x = Constant, y = Linear, z = Quadratic, w = Unused.
  };
  /*!
   * @brief Per-frame scene parameters cached during BeginScene.
   */
  struct SceneData {
    Math::Mat4          ViewProjectionMatrix;
    DirectionalLight    SunLight;
    EnvironmentLighting Environment;
  };
  /*!
   * @brief Static Metal rendering engine managing command buffers, passes, and global GPU state.
   */
  class Renderer {
  public:
    static constexpr uint32_t kMaxPointLights = 64; ///< Maximum active point lights per frame.
    /*!
     * @brief Initializes global Metal buffers, samplers, framebuffers, and pipelines.
     */
    static void Init();
    /*!
     * @brief Releases all GPU resources owned by the Renderer.
     */
    static void Shutdown();
    /*!
     * @brief Begins the main HDR forward rendering pass.
     * @param camera Active perspective camera.
     * @param sun Primary directional light.
     * @param env Global environment ambient settings.
     * @param target Optional target framebuffer (defaults to swapchain if nullptr).
     * @param pointLights Span of active point lights in the scene.
     */
    static void BeginScene(const PerspectiveCamera& camera,
                           const DirectionalLight& sun,
                           const EnvironmentLighting& env,
                           const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& target = nullptr,
                           std::span<const GPUPointLight> pointLights = {});
    /*!
     * @brief Begins the depth-only directional shadow map pass.
     * @param sun Primary directional light.
     * @param shadowTarget Depth-only shadow framebuffer.
     */
    static void BeginShadowPass(const DirectionalLight& sun, const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& shadowTarget);
    /*!
     * @brief Submits an instanced draw call for a mesh and material batch.
     * @param pipeline Pipeline state to bind.
     * @param material Surface material (ignored during shadow pass).
     * @param mesh Geometry mesh to draw.
     * @param instances Span of world and normal matrices for all instances.
     */
    static void SubmitInstanced(const std::shared_ptr<Pipeline>& pipeline,
                                const std::shared_ptr<Material>& material,
                                const std::shared_ptr<Mesh>& mesh,
                                std::span<const InstanceData> instances);
    /*!
     * @brief Ends the current render pass, executes compute bloom and tonemapping, and presents the drawable.
     */
    static void EndScene();
    static void SetShadowMap(const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& shadowFramebuffer);
    static void SetEnvironmentMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& textureCube);
    static void SetIrradianceMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& irradianceMap);
    static void SetSkyboxToIrradiance(bool useIrradiance);
    static void SetEnvironmentMode(EnvironmentMode mode);
    [[nodiscard]] static EnvironmentMode GetEnvironmentMode() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> GetStandardPipeline() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> GetTransparentPipeline() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> GetShadowPipeline() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> GetHDRFramebuffer() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> GetShadowMapFramebuffer() noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static BufferLayout GetStandardMeshLayout() noexcept;
    static void OnWindowResize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height);
    [[nodiscard]] static BloomPass& GetBloomPass() noexcept;
    static void SetExposure(float exposure) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static float GetExposure() noexcept;
    static void SetIBLEnabled(bool enabled) noexcept;
    [[nodiscard]] static bool IsIBLEnabled() noexcept;
  private:
    struct alignas(256) GPUUniforms {
      Math::Mat4    ViewProjectionMatrix;
      Math::Mat4    LightSpaceMatrix;
      Math::Vec3    CameraPosition;
      Math::Vec3    LightDirection;
      Math::Vec4    DirectionalColor;
      Math::Vec4    AmbientColor;
      uint32_t      PointLightCount{0};
      uint32_t      Padding[3]{0, 0, 0};
      GPUPointLight PointLights[kMaxPointLights];
    };
    struct alignas(256) ShadowUniforms {
      Math::Mat4 LightViewProjectionMatrix;
    };
    struct alignas(16) TonemapUniforms {
      float BloomIntensity{0.8f};
      float Exposure{1.0f};
      float Padding[2]{0.0f, 0.0f};
    };
    struct RendererStorage {
      static constexpr size_t MaxInstancesPerFrame = 16384;
      static constexpr size_t InstanceBufferSize   = MaxInstancesPerFrame * sizeof(InstanceData);
      SceneData                       ActiveScene;
      FrameSynchronizer               Sync{3};
      std::vector<MTL::Buffer*>       UniformBuffers;
      std::vector<MTL::Buffer*>       ShadowUniformBuffers;
      std::vector<MTL::Buffer*>       InstanceBuffers;
      size_t                          CurrentInstanceOffset{0};
      MTL::SamplerState*              ShadowSampler{nullptr};
      MTL::SamplerState*              LinearSampler{nullptr};
      MTL::RenderPipelineState*       TonemapPipeline{nullptr};
      MTL::RenderPipelineState*       SkyboxPipeline{nullptr};
      MTL::DepthStencilState*         SkyboxDepthState{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Pipeline>       StandardPipeline{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Pipeline>       TransparentPipeline{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Pipeline>       ShadowPipeline{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>    HDRFramebuffer{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>    ShadowMap{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>    ActiveShadowMap{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>    ActiveEnvironmentMap{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>    ActiveIrradianceMap{nullptr};
      std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>    ActiveProceduralMap{nullptr};
      EnvironmentMode                 CurrentEnvMode{EnvironmentMode::CrispHDR};
      bool                            EnableIBL{true};
      std::shared_ptr<Texture2D>      DefaultWhiteTexture{nullptr};
      bool                            RenderIrradianceSkybox{false};
      BloomPass                       Bloom;
      float                           Exposure{1.0f};
      std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>    ActiveFramebuffer{nullptr};
      MTL::CommandBuffer*             ActiveCommandBuffer{nullptr};
      MTL::RenderCommandEncoder*      ActiveCommandEncoder{nullptr};
      uint32_t                        ActiveFrameIndex{0};
      bool                            IsShadowPass{false};
    };
    static std::unique_ptr<RendererStorage> s_Storage;
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Renderer/Renderer.mm
/*!
 * @file Renderer.mm
 * @brief Metal implementation of the KanVerse Renderer with opaque, transparent, shadow, IBL, and point light support.
 * @copyright KanVerse Engine
 */
#include "Renderer.hpp"
#include "Renderer/Renderer/RenderCommand.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Log.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Platform/Window/NativeWindow.hpp"
#import <MetalKit/MetalKit.h>
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
#include <QuartzCore/QuartzCore.hpp>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>
namespace KanVerse {
  std::unique_ptr<Renderer::RendererStorage> Renderer::s_Storage = nullptr;
  /*!
   * @brief Allocates triple-buffered uniform/instance buffers, samplers, framebuffers, and pipelines.
   */
  void Renderer::Init() {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(!s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer already initialized!");
    s_Storage = std::make_unique<RendererStorage>();
    MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
    const uint32_t maxFrames = s_Storage->Sync.GetMaxFramesInFlight();
    s_Storage->UniformBuffers.resize(maxFrames);
    s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers.resize(maxFrames);
    s_Storage->InstanceBuffers.resize(maxFrames);
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < maxFrames; ++i) {
      s_Storage->UniformBuffers[i] = device->newBuffer(sizeof(GPUUniforms), MTL::ResourceStorageModeShared);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage->UniformBuffers[i] != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Failed to allocate uniform buffer!");
      s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers[i] = device->newBuffer(sizeof(ShadowUniforms), MTL::ResourceStorageModeShared);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers[i] != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Failed to allocate shadow uniform buffer!");
      s_Storage->InstanceBuffers[i] = device->newBuffer(RendererStorage::InstanceBufferSize, MTL::ResourceStorageModeShared);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage->InstanceBuffers[i] != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Failed to allocate instancing buffer!");
    }
    MTL::SamplerDescriptor* shadowSamplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
    shadowSamplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
    shadowSamplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
    shadowSamplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterNotMipmapped);
    shadowSamplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
    shadowSamplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
    shadowSamplerDesc->setCompareFunction(MTL::CompareFunctionLessEqual);
    s_Storage->ShadowSampler = device->newSamplerState(shadowSamplerDesc);
    shadowSamplerDesc->release();
    MTL::SamplerDescriptor* linearSamplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
    linearSamplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
    linearSamplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
    linearSamplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterLinear);
    linearSamplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
    linearSamplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
    s_Storage->LinearSampler = device->newSamplerState(linearSamplerDesc);
    linearSamplerDesc->release();
    s_Storage->ActiveProceduralMap = TextureCube::CreateProcedural(256);
    s_Storage->Bloom.Init();
    TextureSpecification whiteSpec{.Width = 1, .Height = 1, .GenerateMipmaps = false};
    s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture = Texture2D::Create(whiteSpec);
    uint32_t whitePixel = 0xFFFFFFFF;
    s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->SetData(&whitePixel, sizeof(uint32_t));
    MTL::Library* defaultLib = device->newDefaultLibrary();
    if (defaultLib) {
      auto vertStr = NS::String::string("tonemap_vertex", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      auto fragStr = NS::String::string("tonemap_fragment", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      MTL::Function* vertFunc = defaultLib->newFunction(vertStr);
      MTL::Function* fragFunc = defaultLib->newFunction(fragStr);
      vertStr->release();
      fragStr->release();
      if (vertFunc && fragFunc) {
        auto pipeDesc = MTL::RenderPipelineDescriptor::alloc()->init();
        pipeDesc->setVertexFunction(vertFunc);
        pipeDesc->setFragmentFunction(fragFunc);
        pipeDesc->colorAttachments()->object(0)->setPixelFormat(MTL::PixelFormatBGRA8Unorm_sRGB);
        pipeDesc->setDepthAttachmentPixelFormat(MTL::PixelFormatDepth32Float);
        NS::Error* err = nullptr;
        s_Storage->TonemapPipeline = device->newRenderPipelineState(pipeDesc, &err);
        pipeDesc->release();
        if (!s_Storage->TonemapPipeline) {
          KV_CORE_ERROR("Failed to compile TonemapPipeline: %s",
                        err ? err->localizedDescription()->utf8String() : "Unknown error");
        }
      }
      if (vertFunc) vertFunc->release();
      if (fragFunc) fragFunc->release();
      auto skyVertStr = NS::String::string("skybox_vertex", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      auto skyFragStr = NS::String::string("skybox_fragment", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      MTL::Function* skyVert = defaultLib->newFunction(skyVertStr);
      MTL::Function* skyFrag = defaultLib->newFunction(skyFragStr);
      skyVertStr->release();
      skyFragStr->release();
      if (skyVert && skyFrag) {
        auto skyDesc = MTL::RenderPipelineDescriptor::alloc()->init();
        skyDesc->setVertexFunction(skyVert);
        skyDesc->setFragmentFunction(skyFrag);
        skyDesc->colorAttachments()->object(0)->setPixelFormat(MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float);
        skyDesc->setDepthAttachmentPixelFormat(MTL::PixelFormatDepth32Float);
        NS::Error* err = nullptr;
        s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline = device->newRenderPipelineState(skyDesc, &err);
        skyDesc->release();
        auto depthDesc = MTL::DepthStencilDescriptor::alloc()->init();
        depthDesc->setDepthCompareFunction(MTL::CompareFunctionLessEqual);
        depthDesc->setDepthWriteEnabled(false);
        s_Storage->SkyboxDepthState = device->newDepthStencilState(depthDesc);
        depthDesc->release();
      }
      if (skyVert) skyVert->release();
      if (skyFrag) skyFrag->release();
      defaultLib->release();
    }
    FramebufferSpecification hdrSpec{
      .Width = 1280, .Height = 720, .Samples = 1,
      .Format = FramebufferPixelFormat::RGBA16Float,
      .SwapChainTarget = false,
      .DepthOnly = false,
      .ClearColor = {0.06f, 0.07f, 0.09f, 1.0f}
    };
    s_Storage->HDRFramebuffer = Framebuffer::Create(hdrSpec);
    FramebufferSpecification shadowSpec{
      .Width = 2048, .Height = 2048, .DepthOnly = true
    };
    s_Storage->ShadowMap = Framebuffer::Create(shadowSpec);
    SetShadowMap(s_Storage->ShadowMap);
    auto standardShader = Shader::CreateFromDefaultLibrary("vertex_main", "fragment_main");
    PipelineSpecification pipelineSpec;
    pipelineSpec.ShaderProgram    = standardShader;
    pipelineSpec.UseHDRTarget     = true;
    pipelineSpec.EnableDepthWrite = true;
    pipelineSpec.EnableCulling    = true;
    pipelineSpec.Layout           = GetStandardMeshLayout();
    s_Storage->StandardPipeline   = Pipeline::Create(pipelineSpec);
    PipelineSpecification transparentSpec;
    transparentSpec.ShaderProgram    = standardShader;
    transparentSpec.UseHDRTarget     = true;
    transparentSpec.EnableDepthWrite = false;
    transparentSpec.EnableCulling    = false;
    transparentSpec.Layout           = GetStandardMeshLayout();
    s_Storage->TransparentPipeline   = Pipeline::Create(transparentSpec);
    PipelineSpecification shadowSpecPipe;
    shadowSpecPipe.ShaderProgram = Shader::CreateVertexOnly("shadow_vertex_main");
    shadowSpecPipe.Layout        = GetStandardMeshLayout();
    shadowSpecPipe.UseHDRTarget  = false;
    s_Storage->ShadowPipeline    = Pipeline::Create(shadowSpecPipe);
    KV_CORE_INFO("Renderer initialized successfully with Compute Bloom, ACES tonemapping, Transparency, Point Lights, and IBL.");
  }
  /*!
   * @brief Tears down all GPU pipelines, buffers, and framebuffers.
   */
  void Renderer::Shutdown() {
    if (!s_Storage) return;
    if (s_Storage->TonemapPipeline) {
      s_Storage->TonemapPipeline->release();
      s_Storage->TonemapPipeline = nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->LinearSampler) {
      s_Storage->LinearSampler->release();
      s_Storage->LinearSampler = nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->ShadowSampler) {
      s_Storage->ShadowSampler->release();
      s_Storage->ShadowSampler = nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline) {
      s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline->release();
      s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline = nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->SkyboxDepthState) {
      s_Storage->SkyboxDepthState->release();
      s_Storage->SkyboxDepthState = nullptr;
    }
    for (auto* buf : s_Storage->UniformBuffers) if (buf) buf->release();
    for (auto* buf : s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers) if (buf) buf->release();
    for (auto* buf : s_Storage->InstanceBuffers) if (buf) buf->release();
    s_Storage->UniformBuffers.clear();
    s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers.clear();
    s_Storage->InstanceBuffers.clear();
    s_Storage->StandardPipeline.reset();
    s_Storage->TransparentPipeline.reset();
    s_Storage->ShadowPipeline.reset();
    s_Storage->HDRFramebuffer.reset();
    s_Storage->ShadowMap.reset();
    s_Storage->ActiveShadowMap.reset();
    s_Storage.reset();
  }
  BufferLayout Renderer::GetStandardMeshLayout() noexcept {
    return {
      { ShaderDataType::Float3, "position" },
      { ShaderDataType::Float3, "normal" },
      { ShaderDataType::Float4, "color" },
      { ShaderDataType::Float2, "texCoord" }
    };
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> Renderer::GetStandardPipeline() noexcept {
    return s_Storage->StandardPipeline;
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> Renderer::GetTransparentPipeline() noexcept {
    return s_Storage->TransparentPipeline;
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Pipeline> Renderer::GetShadowPipeline() noexcept {
    return s_Storage->ShadowPipeline;
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> Renderer::GetHDRFramebuffer() noexcept {
    return s_Storage->HDRFramebuffer;
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer> Renderer::GetShadowMapFramebuffer() noexcept {
    return s_Storage->ShadowMap;
  }
  void Renderer::OnWindowResize(uint32_t width, uint32_t height) {
    if (s_Storage && s_Storage->HDRFramebuffer) {
      s_Storage->HDRFramebuffer->Resize(width, height);
    }
  }
  BloomPass& Renderer::GetBloomPass() noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    return s_Storage->Bloom;
  }
  void Renderer::SetExposure(float exposure) noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->Exposure = exposure;
  }
  float Renderer::GetExposure() noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    return s_Storage->Exposure;
  }
  void Renderer::SetIBLEnabled(bool enabled) noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->EnableIBL = enabled;
    KV_CORE_INFO("Renderer: Image-Based Lighting (IBL) toggled {}", enabled ? "ON" : "OFF");
  }
  bool Renderer::IsIBLEnabled() noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    return s_Storage->EnableIBL;
  }
  void Renderer::SetShadowMap(const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& shadowFramebuffer) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->ActiveShadowMap = shadowFramebuffer;
  }
  void Renderer::SetEnvironmentMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& textureCube) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->ActiveEnvironmentMap = textureCube;
  }
  void Renderer::SetIrradianceMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& irradianceMap) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->ActiveIrradianceMap = irradianceMap;
  }
  void Renderer::SetSkyboxToIrradiance(bool useIrradiance) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->RenderIrradianceSkybox = useIrradiance;
  }
  void Renderer::SetEnvironmentMode(EnvironmentMode mode) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->CurrentEnvMode = mode;
    switch (mode) {
      case EnvironmentMode::CrispHDR:
        SetSkyboxToIrradiance(false);
        break;
      case EnvironmentMode::Irradiance:
        SetSkyboxToIrradiance(true);
        break;
      case EnvironmentMode::Procedural:
        SetSkyboxToIrradiance(false);
        break;
    }
    KV_CORE_INFO("Renderer: Environment mode updated successfully.");
  }
  EnvironmentMode Renderer::GetEnvironmentMode() noexcept {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    return s_Storage->CurrentEnvMode;
  }
  void Renderer::BeginShadowPass(const DirectionalLight& sun,
                                 const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& shadowTarget) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(shadowTarget != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Shadow render target cannot be null!");
    s_Storage->IsShadowPass = true;
    s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer = shadowTarget;
    s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex = s_Storage->Sync.BeginFrame();
    s_Storage->CurrentInstanceOffset = 0;
    auto& app = Application::Get();
    s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer = app.GetRenderContext()->GetCommandQueue()->commandBuffer();
    s_Storage->Sync.AttachToCommandBuffer(s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer);
    ShadowUniforms shadowUniforms{};
    shadowUniforms.LightViewProjectionMatrix = sun.GetViewProjectionMatrix();
    MTL::Buffer* shadowBuf = s_Storage->ShadowUniformBuffers[s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex];
    std::memcpy(shadowBuf->contents(), &shadowUniforms, sizeof(ShadowUniforms));
    s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder = s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->renderCommandEncoder(
                                                                                           shadowTarget->GetRenderPassDescriptor()
                                                                                           );
    if (s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder) {
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setVertexBuffer(shadowBuf, 0, 1);
    }
  }
  /*!
   * @brief Begins the primary scene forward pass and uploads global lighting & point light uniforms.
   * @param camera Active perspective camera.
   * @param sun Primary directional sun light.
   * @param env Ambient environment lighting settings.
   * @param target Optional HDR framebuffer target.
   * @param pointLights Span of active GPU point lights for indoor/local illumination.
   */
  void Renderer::BeginScene(const PerspectiveCamera& camera,
                            const DirectionalLight& sun,
                            const EnvironmentLighting& env,
                            const std::shared_ptr<Framebuffer>& target,
                            std::span<const GPUPointLight> pointLights) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    s_Storage->IsShadowPass                     = false;
    s_Storage->ActiveScene.ViewProjectionMatrix = camera.GetViewProjectionMatrix();
    s_Storage->ActiveScene.SunLight             = sun;
    s_Storage->ActiveScene.Environment          = env;
    s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer                = target;
    if (!s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer) {
      s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex = s_Storage->Sync.BeginFrame();
      s_Storage->CurrentInstanceOffset = 0;
      auto& app = Application::Get();
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer = app.GetRenderContext()->GetCommandQueue()->commandBuffer();
      s_Storage->Sync.AttachToCommandBuffer(s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer);
    }
    const auto& sunLight = s_Storage->ActiveScene.SunLight;
    const Math::Vec4 directionalColor = Math::MakeVec4(
                                                       sunLight.GetColor().x * sunLight.GetIntensity(),
                                                       sunLight.GetColor().y * sunLight.GetIntensity(),
                                                       sunLight.GetColor().z * sunLight.GetIntensity(),
                                                       1.0f
                                                       );
    GPUUniforms uniforms = {};
    uniforms.ViewProjectionMatrix = camera.GetViewProjectionMatrix();
    uniforms.LightSpaceMatrix     = sunLight.GetViewProjectionMatrix();
    uniforms.CameraPosition       = camera.GetPosition();
    uniforms.LightDirection       = sunLight.GetDirection();
    uniforms.DirectionalColor     = directionalColor;
    uniforms.AmbientColor         = env.GetEffectiveAmbient();
    const uint32_t numPointLights = static_cast<uint32_t>(
                                                          std::min<size_t>(pointLights.size(), kMaxPointLights)
                                                          );
    uniforms.PointLightCount = numPointLights;
    if (numPointLights > 0) {
      std::memcpy(uniforms.PointLights, pointLights.data(), numPointLights * sizeof(GPUPointLight));
    }
    MTL::Buffer* uniformBuffer = s_Storage->UniformBuffers[s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex];
    std::memcpy(uniformBuffer->contents(), &uniforms, sizeof(GPUUniforms));
    if (target) {
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder = s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->renderCommandEncoder(
                                                                                             target->GetRenderPassDescriptor()
                                                                                             );
    } else {
      auto& app = Application::Get();
      auto* nativeWin = static_cast<const NativeWindow*>(&app.GetWindow());
      MTKView* metalView = (__bridge MTKView*)nativeWin->GetNativeView();
      MTL::RenderPassDescriptor* passDesc = (__bridge MTL::RenderPassDescriptor*)metalView.currentRenderPassDescriptor;
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder = passDesc ? s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->renderCommandEncoder(passDesc) : nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder) {
      if (!s_Storage->IsShadowPass && s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline) {
        std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> skyboxTex = nullptr;
        if (s_Storage->EnableIBL) {
          if (s_Storage->CurrentEnvMode == EnvironmentMode::Procedural) {
            skyboxTex = s_Storage->ActiveProceduralMap;
          } else {
            skyboxTex = s_Storage->RenderIrradianceSkybox ? s_Storage->ActiveIrradianceMap : s_Storage->ActiveEnvironmentMap;
          }
        }
        if (skyboxTex && s_Storage->LinearSampler) {
          s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setRenderPipelineState(s_Storage->SkyboxPipeline);
          s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setDepthStencilState(s_Storage->SkyboxDepthState);
          struct SkyboxUniforms {
            Math::Mat4 InverseView;
            Math::Mat4 InverseProjection;
          };
          SkyboxUniforms skyUbo{
            Math::Inverse(camera.GetViewMatrix()),
            Math::Inverse(camera.GetProjection())
          };
          s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setVertexBytes(&skyUbo, sizeof(SkyboxUniforms), 0);
          skyboxTex->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 0, 0);
          s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->drawPrimitives(MTL::PrimitiveTypeTriangle, (NS::UInteger)0, (NS::UInteger)3);
        }
      }
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setVertexBuffer(uniformBuffer, 0, 1);
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setFragmentBuffer(uniformBuffer, 0, 1);
      if (s_Storage->ActiveShadowMap && s_Storage->ShadowSampler) {
        s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setFragmentTexture(s_Storage->ActiveShadowMap->GetDepthAttachment(), 6);
        s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setFragmentSamplerState(s_Storage->ShadowSampler, 1);
      }
      if (s_Storage->EnableIBL) {
        std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> iblMap = s_Storage->ActiveEnvironmentMap;
        if (s_Storage->CurrentEnvMode == EnvironmentMode::Procedural) {
          iblMap = s_Storage->ActiveProceduralMap;
        }
        if (iblMap && s_Storage->LinearSampler) {
          iblMap->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 7, 2);
          iblMap->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 8, 2);
        } else {
          if (s_Storage->ActiveEnvironmentMap && s_Storage->LinearSampler) {
            s_Storage->ActiveEnvironmentMap->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 7, 2);
          }
          if (s_Storage->ActiveIrradianceMap && s_Storage->LinearSampler) {
            s_Storage->ActiveIrradianceMap->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 8, 2);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  void Renderer::SubmitInstanced(const std::shared_ptr<Pipeline>& pipeline,
                                 const std::shared_ptr<Material>& material,
                                 const std::shared_ptr<Mesh>& mesh,
                                 std::span<const InstanceData> instances) {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    if (!s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder || instances.empty()) return;
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(pipeline != nullptr, "[KanVerse Renderer] Pipeline is null!");
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(mesh != nullptr,     "[KanVerse Renderer] Mesh is null!");
    const size_t count = instances.size();
    const size_t bytesRequired = count * sizeof(InstanceData);
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage->CurrentInstanceOffset + bytesRequired <= RendererStorage::InstanceBufferSize,
                   "[KanVerse Renderer] Exceeded frame instancing buffer capacity!");
    MTL::Buffer* currentInstanceBuffer = s_Storage->InstanceBuffers[s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex];
    const size_t bufferOffset = s_Storage->CurrentInstanceOffset;
    uint8_t* dest = static_cast<uint8_t*>(currentInstanceBuffer->contents()) + bufferOffset;
    std::memcpy(dest, instances.data(), bytesRequired);
    s_Storage->CurrentInstanceOffset += bytesRequired;
    if (!s_Storage->IsShadowPass && material) {
      material->Sync(s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex);
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setFragmentBuffer(material->GetUniformBuffer(s_Storage->ActiveFrameIndex), 0, 2);
      if (material->GetAlbedoTexture()) {
        material->GetAlbedoTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 0, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 0, 0);
      }
      if (material->GetNormalTexture()) {
        material->GetNormalTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 1, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 1, 0);
      }
      if (material->GetRoughnessTexture()) {
        material->GetRoughnessTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 2, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 2, 0);
      }
      if (material->GetMetallicTexture()) {
        material->GetMetallicTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 3, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 3, 0);
      }
      if (material->GetAOTexture()) {
        material->GetAOTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 4, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 4, 0);
      }
      if (material->GetHeightTexture()) {
        material->GetHeightTexture()->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 5, 0);
      } else if (s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture) {
        s_Storage->DefaultWhiteTexture->Bind(s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder, 5, 0);
      }
    }
    s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->setVertexBuffer(currentInstanceBuffer, bufferOffset, 3);
    for (const auto& submesh : mesh->GetSubmeshes()) {
      (void)submesh;
      RenderCommand::DrawIndexedInstanced(
                                          s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder,
                                          pipeline,
                                          mesh->GetVertexBuffer(),
                                          mesh->GetIndexBuffer(),
                                          static_cast<uint32_t>(count)
                                          );
    }
  }
  void Renderer::EndScene() {
    KV_CORE_ASSERT(s_Storage, "[KanVerse Renderer] Renderer not initialized!");
    if (s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder) {
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder->endEncoding();
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandEncoder = nullptr;
    }
    if (s_Storage->IsShadowPass) {
      s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer = nullptr;
      return;
    }
    auto& app = Application::Get();
    auto* nativeWin = static_cast<const NativeWindow*>(&app.GetWindow());
    MTKView* metalView = (__bridge MTKView*)nativeWin->GetNativeView();
    if (s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer) {
      id<CAMetalDrawable> currentDrawable = nil;
      if (metalView && metalView.bounds.size.width > 1.0 && metalView.bounds.size.height > 1.0) {
        currentDrawable = metalView.currentDrawable;
      }
      if (s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer && currentDrawable && s_Storage->TonemapPipeline) {
        MTL::Texture* hdrColor = s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer->GetColorAttachment(0);
        if (hdrColor) {
          s_Storage->Bloom.Resize(static_cast<uint32_t>(hdrColor->width()),
                                  static_cast<uint32_t>(hdrColor->height()));
          s_Storage->Bloom.Execute(s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer, hdrColor);
        }
        MTL::RenderPassDescriptor* displayPassDesc = (__bridge MTL::RenderPassDescriptor*)metalView.currentRenderPassDescriptor;
        if (displayPassDesc) {
          MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* tonemapEncoder = s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->renderCommandEncoder(displayPassDesc);
          if (tonemapEncoder) {
            tonemapEncoder->setRenderPipelineState(s_Storage->TonemapPipeline);
            tonemapEncoder->setFragmentTexture(hdrColor, 0);
            tonemapEncoder->setFragmentTexture(s_Storage->Bloom.GetBloomTexture(), 1);
            tonemapEncoder->setFragmentSamplerState(s_Storage->LinearSampler, 0);
            TonemapUniforms tonemapUniforms;
            tonemapUniforms.BloomIntensity = s_Storage->Bloom.GetIntensity();
            tonemapUniforms.Exposure       = s_Storage->Exposure;
            tonemapEncoder->setFragmentBytes(&tonemapUniforms, sizeof(TonemapUniforms), 0);
            tonemapEncoder->drawPrimitives(MTL::PrimitiveTypeTriangle, (NS::UInteger)0, (NS::UInteger)3);
            tonemapEncoder->endEncoding();
          }
        }
      }
      if (currentDrawable) {
        s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->presentDrawable((__bridge CA::MetalDrawable*)currentDrawable);
      }
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer->commit();
      s_Storage->ActiveCommandBuffer = nullptr;
    }
    s_Storage->ActiveFramebuffer = nullptr;
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Textures/Texture.hpp
/*!
 * @file Texture.hpp
 * @brief Base abstract contract representing hardware-accelerated GPU textures.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <memory>
namespace MTL {
  class Texture;
  class SamplerState;
  class RenderCommandEncoder;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @class Texture
   * @brief Abstract base class for GPU texture resources.
   */
  class Texture {
  public:
    virtual ~Texture() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries texture width in texels.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetWidth() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Queries texture height in texels.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetHeight() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Binds texture and sampler states to a render encoder at specified binding slots.
     * @param encoder Active Metal command encoder.
     * @param textureSlot Fragment shader texture slot index.
     * @param samplerSlot Fragment shader sampler slot index.
     */
    virtual void Bind(MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* encoder, uint32_t textureSlot = 0, uint32_t samplerSlot = 0) const = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses underlying native Metal texture pointer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Texture* GetNativeTexture() const noexcept = 0;
    /*!
     * @brief Accesses underlying native Metal sampler state pointer.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::SamplerState* GetNativeSampler() const noexcept = 0;
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Textures/Texture2D.hpp
/*!
 * @file Texture2D.hpp
 * @brief 2D Texture asset abstraction encapsulating Metal texture objects and samplers.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include <cstdint>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
namespace MTL {
  class Texture;
  class SamplerState;
  class RenderCommandEncoder;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /**
   * @brief Configuration specification for creating a 2D Texture.
   */
  struct TextureSpecification {
    uint32_t Width{1};
    uint32_t Height{1};
    bool     GenerateMipmaps{false};
    /**
     * @brief Determines the color space of the texture.
     * True (sRGB) for color textures (Albedo, Emissive).
     * False (Linear) for data textures (Normal, Roughness, Metallic, AO, Height).
     */
    bool     sRGB{true};
  };
  /**
   * @brief Cross-platform interface for an Engine 2D Texture.
   */
  class Texture2D {
  public:
    virtual ~Texture2D() = default;
    /**
     * @brief Creates a 2D texture based on a manual specification.
     * @param spec The specification dictating dimensions and format.
     * @return A shared pointer to the created texture.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> Create(const TextureSpecification& spec);
    /**
     * @brief Loads and creates a 2D texture from an image file.
     * @param filepath The absolute or relative path to the image file.
     * @param sRGB Whether to load the image in sRGB color space.
     * @return A shared pointer to the created texture, or nullptr if loading failed.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> Create(const std::string& filepath, bool sRGB = true);
    /**
     * @brief Generates a default checkerboard texture (useful as a fallback for missing textures).
     * @param resolution The width and height of the square texture.
     * @return A shared pointer to the checkerboard texture.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> CreateCheckerboard(uint32_t resolution = 256);
    /**
     * @brief Uploads raw pixel data to the GPU texture.
     * @param data Pointer to the raw pixel buffer.
     * @param size The size of the buffer in bytes.
     */
    virtual void SetData(const void* data, size_t size) = 0;
    /**
     * @brief Binds the texture and its sampler to the current render pipeline.
     * @param encoder The Metal render command encoder.
     * @param textureSlot The slot index to bind the texture to.
     * @param samplerSlot The slot index to bind the sampler state to.
     */
    virtual void Bind(MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* encoder,
                      uint32_t textureSlot = 0,
                      uint32_t samplerSlot = 0) const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetWidth() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetHeight() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Texture* GetNativeTexture() const noexcept = 0;
  };
}
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Textures/Texture2D.mm
/*!
 * @file Texture2D.mm
 * @brief Implementation of Metal 2D texture allocation, image loading, and command binding.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Textures/Texture2D.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Log.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
#include <QuartzCore/QuartzCore.hpp>
#import <Metal/Metal.h>
#import <CoreGraphics/CoreGraphics.h>
#import <ImageIO/ImageIO.h>
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
#include <vector>
namespace KanVerse {
  /**
   * @brief Metal API specific implementation of Texture2D.
   */
  class MetalTexture2D : public Texture2D {
  public:
    explicit MetalTexture2D(const TextureSpecification& spec)
    : m_Specification(spec), m_Width(spec.Width), m_Height(spec.Height) {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Width > 0 && m_Height > 0, "[KanVerse Texture] Texture dimensions must be positive non-zero!");
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      // Determine correct pixel format based on sRGB flag
      MTL::PixelFormat pixelFormat = m_Specification.sRGB ?
      MTL::PixelFormatRGBA8Unorm_sRGB :
      MTL::PixelFormatRGBA8Unorm;
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* desc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::texture2DDescriptor(
                                                                                 pixelFormat,
                                                                                 m_Width,
                                                                                 m_Height,
                                                                                 m_Specification.GenerateMipmaps
                                                                                 );
      desc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
      desc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModeShared);
      m_Texture = device->newTexture(desc);
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Texture != nullptr, "[KanVerse Texture] Failed to allocate MTL::Texture!");
      const size_t vramBytes = m_Width * m_Height * 4;
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(vramBytes);
      MTL::SamplerDescriptor* samplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
      samplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeRepeat);
      samplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeRepeat);
      m_Sampler = device->newSamplerState(samplerDesc);
      samplerDesc->release();
    }
    ~MetalTexture2D() override {
      if (m_Sampler) {
        m_Sampler->release();
        m_Sampler = nullptr;
      }
      if (m_Texture) {
        m_Texture->release();
        m_Texture = nullptr;
      }
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(0);
    }
    void SetData(const void* data, size_t size) override {
      const size_t expectedBytes = m_Width * m_Height * 4;
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(size == expectedBytes, "[KanVerse Texture] Upload buffer byte size mismatch!");
      const MTL::Region region = MTL::Region(0, 0, 0, m_Width, m_Height, 1);
      const NSUInteger bytesPerRow = m_Width * 4;
      m_Texture->replaceRegion(region, 0, data, bytesPerRow);
    }
    void Bind(MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* encoder, uint32_t textureSlot, uint32_t samplerSlot) const override {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(encoder != nullptr, "[KanVerse Texture] Command encoder cannot be null during bind!");
      encoder->setFragmentTexture(m_Texture, textureSlot);
      encoder->setFragmentSamplerState(m_Sampler, samplerSlot);
    }
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t GetWidth() const noexcept override { return m_Width; }
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t GetHeight() const noexcept override { return m_Height; }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Texture* GetNativeTexture() const noexcept override { return m_Texture; }
  private:
    TextureSpecification   m_Specification;
    uint32_t               m_Width{0};
    uint32_t               m_Height{0};
    MTL::Texture*          m_Texture{nullptr};
    MTL::SamplerState*     m_Sampler{nullptr};
    ScopedMemoryAllocation m_MemoryGuard{};
  };
  std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> Texture2D::Create(const TextureSpecification& spec) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTexture2D>(spec);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> Texture2D::Create(const std::string& filepath, bool sRGB) {
    std::vector<uint8_t> rawPixels;
    uint32_t width = 0;
    uint32_t height = 0;
    @autoreleasepool {
      NSString* pathString = [NSString stringWithUTF8String:filepath.c_str()];
      NSURL* fileURL = [NSURL fileURLWithPath:pathString];
      CGImageSourceRef imageSource = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL((__bridge CFURLRef)fileURL, NULL);
      if (!imageSource) {
        KV_CORE_ERROR("Texture2D: Failed to open image file at path: '{}'", filepath);
        return nullptr;
      }
      CGImageRef image = CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(imageSource, 0, NULL);
      CFRelease(imageSource);
      if (!image) {
        KV_CORE_ERROR("Texture2D: Failed to decode image data from file: '{}'", filepath);
        return nullptr;
      }
      width  = static_cast<uint32_t>(CGImageGetWidth(image));
      height = static_cast<uint32_t>(CGImageGetHeight(image));
      rawPixels.resize(width * height * 4);
      CGColorSpaceRef colorSpace = CGColorSpaceCreateWithName(kCGColorSpaceSRGB);
      CGContextRef context = CGBitmapContextCreate(
                                                   rawPixels.data(),
                                                   width,
                                                   height,
                                                   8,
                                                   width * 4,
                                                   colorSpace,
                                                   static_cast<uint32_t>(kCGImageAlphaPremultipliedLast) | static_cast<uint32_t>(kCGBitmapByteOrder32Big)
                                                   );
      CGColorSpaceRelease(colorSpace);
      if (!context) {
        CGImageRelease(image);
        KV_CORE_ERROR("Texture2D: Failed to create CGBitmapContext for '{}'", filepath);
        return nullptr;
      }
      CGRect rect = CGRectMake(0, 0, width, height);
      CGContextDrawImage(context, rect, image);
      CGContextRelease(context);
      CGImageRelease(image);
    }
    if (rawPixels.empty() || width == 0 || height == 0) {
      return nullptr;
    }
    // Pass the format flag down to the specification
    TextureSpecification spec{
      .Width = width,
      .Height = height,
      .GenerateMipmaps = false,
      .sRGB = sRGB
    };
    auto texture = std::make_shared<MetalTexture2D>(spec);
    texture->SetData(rawPixels.data(), rawPixels.size());
    KV_CORE_INFO("Texture2D: Successfully loaded '{}' ({}x{} RGBA, sRGB: {}, {:.2f} MB)",
                 filepath, width, height, sRGB,
                 static_cast<double>(rawPixels.size()) / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    return texture;
  }
  std::shared_ptr<Texture2D> Texture2D::CreateCheckerboard(uint32_t resolution) {
    TextureSpecification spec{
      .Width = resolution,
      .Height = resolution,
      .GenerateMipmaps = false,
      .sRGB = true // Checkerboard acts as a color texture
    };
    auto texture = std::make_shared<MetalTexture2D>(spec);
    std::vector<uint32_t> pixels(resolution * resolution);
    constexpr uint32_t TileSize = 16;
    for (uint32_t y = 0; y < resolution; ++y) {
      for (uint32_t x = 0; x < resolution; ++x) {
        const bool isWhite = ((x / TileSize) % 2) == ((y / TileSize) % 2);
        pixels[y * resolution + x] = isWhite ? 0xFFFFFFFF : 0xFF222222;
      }
    }
    texture->SetData(pixels.data(), pixels.size() * sizeof(uint32_t));
    return texture;
  }
}
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Textures/TextureCube.hpp
/*!
 * @file TextureCube.hpp
 * @brief Abstract contract representing hardware-accelerated GPU cubemap textures.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#pragma once
#include <simd/simd.h>
namespace MTL {
  class Texture;
  class SamplerState;
  class RenderCommandEncoder;
}
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @struct ProceduralSkyParams
   * @brief Configuration controlling procedural atmospheric gradients and the physical sun disc.
   */
  struct ProceduralSkyParams {
    simd::float3 ZenithColor{0.25f, 0.50f, 0.95f};      ///< Deep sky color overhead
    simd::float3 HorizonColor{0.70f, 0.82f, 0.98f};     ///< Atmospheric haze along the horizon
    simd::float3 GroundColor{0.18f, 0.20f, 0.24f};      ///< Ground reflectance below horizon
    simd::float3 SunDirection{-0.4f, -1.0f, -0.3f};     ///< Incident sunlight vector pointing down
    simd::float3 SunColor{1.0f, 0.95f, 0.88f};          ///< Radiance tint of the sun disc
    float        SunIntensity{12.0f};                   ///< High dynamic range intensity for bloom flare
    float        SunDiscSize{0.035f};                   ///< Angular radius of the sharp core disc
    float        SunGlowSize{0.32f};                    ///< Soft halo/corona radius around the disc
  };
  class TextureCube {
  public:
    virtual ~TextureCube() = default;
    /*!
     * @brief Factory creating a Cubemap texture from 6 individual image files (+X, -X, +Y, -Y, +Z, -Z).
     * @param faces Vector of 6 file paths.
     * @return Shared pointer to initialized TextureCube, or nullptr on failure.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> Create(const std::vector<std::string>& faces);
    /*!
     * @brief Factory procedurally synthesizing a default sky-to-ground environment cubemap.
     * @param resolution Resolution of each square face (e.g., 256 pixels).
     * @return Shared pointer to initialized procedural TextureCube.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> CreateProcedural(uint32_t resolution = 256);
    /*!
     * @brief Factory procedurally synthesizing a sky environment with customizable sun and colors.
     * @param params Sky colors, sun direction, and intensity.
     * @param resolution Resolution of each square face (e.g., 256 pixels).
     * @return Shared pointer to initialized procedural TextureCube.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> CreateProcedural(const ProceduralSkyParams& params, uint32_t resolution = 256);
    /*!
     * @brief Factory creating a Cubemap texture from a single equirectangular .hdr image via GPU Compute.
     * @param filepath Path to the .hdr image file.
     * @param resolution Target resolution of each cubemap face (default: 512).
     * @return Shared pointer to initialized HDR TextureCube.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> CreateFromEquirectangular(const std::string& filepath, uint32_t resolution = 512);
    /*!
     * @brief Convolutes an existing environment map to generate a diffuse Irradiance map.
     * @param sourceEnvMap The crisp source cubemap to sample from.
     * @param resolution Resolution of the output irradiance map (defaults to 32 for extreme blur).
     * @return Shared pointer to the new blurred TextureCube.
     */
    [[nodiscard]] static std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> CreateIrradianceMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& sourceEnvMap, uint32_t resolution = 32);
    virtual void Bind(MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* encoder,
                      uint32_t textureSlot = 0,
                      uint32_t samplerSlot = 0) const = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual uint32_t GetResolution() const noexcept = 0;
    [[nodiscard]] virtual MTL::Texture* GetNativeTexture() const noexcept = 0;
  };
} // namespace KanVerse
// FILE: KanVerse_Engine/Renderer/Textures/TextureCube.mm
/*!
 * @file TextureCube.mm
 * @brief Implementation of Metal cubemap texture allocation, image decoding, and procedural generation.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include "Renderer/Textures/TextureCube.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Log.hpp"
#include "Core/Diagnostics/Assert.hpp"
#include "Core/Application/Application.hpp"
#include "Core/Memory/MemoryTracking.hpp"
#include <stb_image.h>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
#import <CoreGraphics/CoreGraphics.h>
#import <ImageIO/ImageIO.h>
#import <Foundation/Foundation.h>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace KanVerse {
  class MetalTextureCube : public TextureCube {
  public:
    explicit MetalTextureCube(const std::vector<std::string>& faces) {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(faces.size() == 6, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Cubemap requires exactly 6 face image paths!");
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Active Metal device is null!");
      uint32_t resolution = 0;
      std::vector<std::vector<uint8_t>> facePixels(6);
      @autoreleasepool {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < 6; ++i) {
          NSString* pathString = [NSString stringWithUTF8String:faces[i].c_str()];
          NSURL* fileURL = [NSURL fileURLWithPath:pathString];
          CGImageSourceRef imageSource = CGImageSourceCreateWithURL((__bridge CFURLRef)fileURL, NULL);
          if (!imageSource) {
            KV_CORE_ERROR("TextureCube: Failed to open face image at '{}'", faces[i]);
            return;
          }
          CGImageRef image = CGImageSourceCreateImageAtIndex(imageSource, 0, NULL);
          CFRelease(imageSource);
          if (!image) {
            KV_CORE_ERROR("TextureCube: Failed to decode face image at '{}'", faces[i]);
            return;
          }
          const uint32_t w = static_cast<uint32_t>(CGImageGetWidth(image));
          const uint32_t h = static_cast<uint32_t>(CGImageGetHeight(image));
          if (i == 0) {
            resolution = w;
          } else {
            KV_CORE_ASSERT(w == resolution && h == resolution, "[KanVerse TextureCube] All cubemap faces must be square and matching dimensions!");
          }
          facePixels[i].resize(resolution * resolution * 4);
          CGColorSpaceRef colorSpace = CGColorSpaceCreateWithName(kCGColorSpaceSRGB);
          CGContextRef context = CGBitmapContextCreate(
                                                       facePixels[i].data(),
                                                       resolution,
                                                       resolution,
                                                       8,
                                                       resolution * 4,
                                                       colorSpace,
                                                       static_cast<uint32_t>(kCGImageAlphaPremultipliedLast) | static_cast<uint32_t>(kCGBitmapByteOrder32Big)
                                                       );
          CGColorSpaceRelease(colorSpace);
          if (context) {
            CGRect rect = CGRectMake(0, 0, resolution, resolution);
            CGContextDrawImage(context, rect, image);
            CGContextRelease(context);
          }
          CGImageRelease(image);
        }
      }
      m_Resolution = resolution;
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* desc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::textureCubeDescriptor(
                                                                                   MTL::PixelFormatRGBA8Unorm_sRGB,
                                                                                   m_Resolution,
                                                                                   false
                                                                                   );
      desc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
      desc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModeShared);
      m_Texture = device->newTexture(desc);
      desc->release();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Texture != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Failed to allocate MTL::Texture for cubemap!");
      for (size_t i = 0; i < 6; ++i) {
        const MTL::Region region = MTL::Region(0, 0, m_Resolution, m_Resolution);
        const NSUInteger bytesPerRow = m_Resolution * 4;
        m_Texture->replaceRegion(region, 0, static_cast<NSUInteger>(i), facePixels[i].data(), bytesPerRow, bytesPerRow * m_Resolution);
      }
      const size_t vramBytes = m_Resolution * m_Resolution * 4 * 6;
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(vramBytes);
      MTL::SamplerDescriptor* samplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
      samplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterNotMipmapped);
      samplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      samplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      m_Sampler = device->newSamplerState(samplerDesc);
      samplerDesc->release();
      KV_CORE_INFO("TextureCube: Successfully loaded cubemap (Resolution: {}x{}, {:.2f} MB)", m_Resolution, m_Resolution, static_cast<double>(vramBytes) / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    }
    explicit MetalTextureCube(const ProceduralSkyParams& params, uint32_t resolution) {
      m_Resolution = resolution;
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Active Metal device is null!");
      // Use RGBA16Float to store high-dynamic-range sun radiance without 8-bit clipping
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* desc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::textureCubeDescriptor(
                                                                                   MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float,
                                                                                   m_Resolution,
                                                                                   false
                                                                                   );
      desc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
      desc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModeShared);
      m_Texture = device->newTexture(desc);
      desc->release();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(m_Texture != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Failed to allocate procedural MTL::Texture for cubemap!");
      // Normal direction vectors corresponding to the 6 cube faces (+Y and -Y aligned)
      const simd::float3 faceDirections[6] = {
        simd_make_float3( 1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f), // 0: +X
        simd_make_float3(-1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f), // 1: -X
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f, -1.0f,  0.0f), // 2: -Y (Zenith in Metal view space)
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f), // 3: +Y (Ground in Metal view space)
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f,  1.0f), // 4: +Z
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f, -1.0f)  // 5: -Z
      };
      const simd::float3 faceU[6] = {
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f, -1.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f,  1.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3(-1.0f,  0.0f,  0.0f)
      };
      const simd::float3 faceV[6] = {
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f,  1.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  0.0f, -1.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f),
        simd_make_float3( 0.0f,  1.0f,  0.0f)
      };
      // Vector pointing toward the sun position in the sky
      const simd::float3 sunPosition = simd_normalize(-params.SunDirection);
      std::vector<uint16_t> facePixelsHalf(m_Resolution * m_Resolution * 4);
      for (size_t face = 0; face < 6; ++face) {
        for (uint32_t y = 0; y < m_Resolution; ++y) {
          float py = (static_cast<float>(y) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(m_Resolution);
          py = py * 2.0f - 1.0f;
          for (uint32_t x = 0; x < m_Resolution; ++x) {
            float px = (static_cast<float>(x) + 0.5f) / static_cast<float>(m_Resolution);
            px = px * 2.0f - 1.0f;
            simd::float3 dir = simd_normalize(faceDirections[face] + (faceU[face] * px) + (faceV[face] * py));
            // Atmospheric gradient: above horizon uses zenith blend; below uses ground blend
            float t = dir.y;
            simd::float3 skyColor;
            if (t >= 0.0f) {
              float factor = std::pow(t, 0.45f);
              skyColor = params.HorizonColor * (1.0f - factor) + params.ZenithColor * factor;
            } else {
              float factor = std::pow(std::clamp(-t, 0.0f, 1.0f), 0.5f);
              skyColor = params.HorizonColor * (1.0f - factor) + params.GroundColor * factor;
            }
            // Calculate directional angular alignment with the sun
            float sunDot = simd_dot(dir, sunPosition);
            if (sunDot > 0.0f && sunPosition.y > -0.05f) {
              // Soft corona halo glow around the sun
              float glowFactor = std::clamp((sunDot - (1.0f - params.SunGlowSize)) / params.SunGlowSize, 0.0f, 1.0f);
              glowFactor = std::pow(glowFactor, 3.5f);
              skyColor += params.SunColor * (glowFactor * params.SunIntensity * 0.25f);
              // Sharp, high-intensity core sun disc
              float discFactor = std::clamp((sunDot - (1.0f - params.SunDiscSize)) / (params.SunDiscSize * 0.15f), 0.0f, 1.0f);
              skyColor += params.SunColor * (discFactor * params.SunIntensity);
            }
            size_t idx = (y * m_Resolution + x) * 4;
            // Store as 16-bit half floats (__fp16) for HDR buffers
            __fp16 r = static_cast<__fp16>(skyColor.x);
            __fp16 g = static_cast<__fp16>(skyColor.y);
            __fp16 b = static_cast<__fp16>(skyColor.z);
            __fp16 a = static_cast<__fp16>(1.0f);
            std::memcpy(&facePixelsHalf[idx + 0], &r, sizeof(__fp16));
            std::memcpy(&facePixelsHalf[idx + 1], &g, sizeof(__fp16));
            std::memcpy(&facePixelsHalf[idx + 2], &b, sizeof(__fp16));
            std::memcpy(&facePixelsHalf[idx + 3], &a, sizeof(__fp16));
          }
        }
        const MTL::Region region = MTL::Region(0, 0, m_Resolution, m_Resolution);
        const NSUInteger bytesPerRow = m_Resolution * 4 * sizeof(uint16_t);
        m_Texture->replaceRegion(region, 0, static_cast<NSUInteger>(face), facePixelsHalf.data(), bytesPerRow, bytesPerRow * m_Resolution);
      }
      const size_t vramBytes = m_Resolution * m_Resolution * 8 * 6;
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(vramBytes);
      MTL::SamplerDescriptor* samplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
      samplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterNotMipmapped);
      samplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      samplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      m_Sampler = device->newSamplerState(samplerDesc);
      samplerDesc->release();
      KV_CORE_INFO("TextureCube: Successfully generated HDR procedural sky with sun disc (Resolution: {}x{}, {:.2f} MB)",
                   m_Resolution, m_Resolution, static_cast<double>(vramBytes) / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    }
    explicit MetalTextureCube(const std::string& filepath, uint32_t resolution) {
      m_Resolution = resolution;
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(device != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Active Metal device is null!");
      int width, height, channels;
      float* data = stbi_loadf(filepath.c_str(), &width, &height, &channels, 4);
      if (!data) {
        KV_CORE_ERROR("TextureCube: Failed to load HDR image '{0}'. Reason: {1}", filepath, stbi_failure_reason());
        KV_CORE_ASSERT(false, "Failed to load Equirectangular HDR map!");
        return;
      }
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* hdrDesc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::texture2DDescriptor(
                                                                                    MTL::PixelFormatRGBA32Float, width, height, false);
      hdrDesc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead);
      hdrDesc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModeShared);
      MTL::Texture* hdrTexture = device->newTexture(hdrDesc);
      hdrDesc->release();
      const MTL::Region hdrRegion = MTL::Region(0, 0, width, height);
      hdrTexture->replaceRegion(hdrRegion, 0, data, width * 4 * sizeof(float));
      stbi_image_free(data);
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* cubeDesc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::textureCubeDescriptor(
                                                                                       MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float, m_Resolution, true);
      cubeDesc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead | MTL::TextureUsageShaderWrite);
      cubeDesc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModePrivate);
      m_Texture = device->newTexture(cubeDesc);
      cubeDesc->release();
      MTL::Library* defaultLib = device->newDefaultLibrary();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(defaultLib, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Failed to load default Metal library!");
      auto funcName = NS::String::string("equirectangular_to_cubemap", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      MTL::Function* computeFunc = defaultLib->newFunction(funcName);
      funcName->release();
      defaultLib->release();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(computeFunc, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Compute kernel 'equirectangular_to_cubemap' not found!");
      NS::Error* error = nullptr;
      MTL::ComputePipelineState* computePipeline = device->newComputePipelineState(computeFunc, &error);
      computeFunc->release();
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(computePipeline, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Failed to create compute pipeline!");
      MTL::CommandQueue* queue = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetCommandQueue();
      MTL::CommandBuffer* cmdBuffer = queue->commandBuffer();
      MTL::ComputeCommandEncoder* encoder = cmdBuffer->computeCommandEncoder();
      encoder->setComputePipelineState(computePipeline);
      MTL::Texture* cubemapArrayView = m_Texture->newTextureView(
                                                                 MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float,
                                                                 MTL::TextureType2DArray,
                                                                 NS::Range::Make(0, 1),
                                                                 NS::Range::Make(0, 6)
                                                                 );
      encoder->setTexture(hdrTexture, 0);
      encoder->setTexture(cubemapArrayView, 1);
      const MTL::Size threadgroupSize(16, 16, 1);
      const MTL::Size threadgroups(
                                   (m_Resolution + threadgroupSize.width - 1) / threadgroupSize.width,
                                   (m_Resolution + threadgroupSize.height - 1) / threadgroupSize.height,
                                   6
                                   );
      encoder->dispatchThreadgroups(threadgroups, threadgroupSize);
      encoder->endEncoding();
      MTL::BlitCommandEncoder* blitEncoder = cmdBuffer->blitCommandEncoder();
      blitEncoder->generateMipmaps(m_Texture);
      blitEncoder->endEncoding();
      cmdBuffer->commit();
      cmdBuffer->waitUntilCompleted();
      cubemapArrayView->release();
      hdrTexture->release();
      computePipeline->release();
      MTL::SamplerDescriptor* samplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
      samplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      samplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      m_Sampler = device->newSamplerState(samplerDesc);
      samplerDesc->release();
      const size_t vramBytes = m_Resolution * m_Resolution * 8 * 6;
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(vramBytes);
      KV_CORE_INFO("TextureCube: Successfully generated HDR Cubemap from '{0}' (Resolution: {1}x{1}, {2:.2f} MB)",
                   filepath, m_Resolution, static_cast<double>(vramBytes) / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    }
    explicit MetalTextureCube(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& sourceEnvMap, uint32_t resolution) {
      m_Resolution = resolution;
      MTL::Device* device = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetDevice();
      MTL::TextureDescriptor* cubeDesc = MTL::TextureDescriptor::textureCubeDescriptor(
                                                                                       MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float, m_Resolution, false);
      cubeDesc->setUsage(MTL::TextureUsageShaderRead | MTL::TextureUsageShaderWrite);
      cubeDesc->setStorageMode(MTL::StorageModePrivate);
      m_Texture = device->newTexture(cubeDesc);
      cubeDesc->release();
      MTL::Library* defaultLib = device->newDefaultLibrary();
      auto funcName = NS::String::string("irradiance_convolution", NS::UTF8StringEncoding);
      MTL::Function* computeFunc = defaultLib->newFunction(funcName);
      funcName->release();
      defaultLib->release();
      NS::Error* error = nullptr;
      MTL::ComputePipelineState* computePipeline = device->newComputePipelineState(computeFunc, &error);
      computeFunc->release();
      MTL::CommandQueue* queue = Application::Get().GetRenderContext()->GetCommandQueue();
      MTL::CommandBuffer* cmdBuffer = queue->commandBuffer();
      MTL::ComputeCommandEncoder* encoder = cmdBuffer->computeCommandEncoder();
      MTL::Texture* cubemapArrayView = m_Texture->newTextureView(
                                                                 MTL::PixelFormatRGBA16Float,
                                                                 MTL::TextureType2DArray,
                                                                 NS::Range::Make(0, 1),
                                                                 NS::Range::Make(0, 6)
                                                                 );
      encoder->setComputePipelineState(computePipeline);
      encoder->setTexture(sourceEnvMap->GetNativeTexture(), 0);
      encoder->setTexture(cubemapArrayView, 1);
      const MTL::Size threadgroupSize(16, 16, 1);
      const MTL::Size threadgroups(
                                   (m_Resolution + threadgroupSize.width - 1) / threadgroupSize.width,
                                   (m_Resolution + threadgroupSize.height - 1) / threadgroupSize.height,
                                   6
                                   );
      encoder->dispatchThreadgroups(threadgroups, threadgroupSize);
      encoder->endEncoding();
      cmdBuffer->commit();
      cmdBuffer->waitUntilCompleted();
      cubemapArrayView->release();
      computePipeline->release();
      MTL::SamplerDescriptor* samplerDesc = MTL::SamplerDescriptor::alloc()->init();
      samplerDesc->setMinFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMagFilter(MTL::SamplerMinMagFilterLinear);
      samplerDesc->setMipFilter(MTL::SamplerMipFilterNotMipmapped);
      samplerDesc->setSAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      samplerDesc->setTAddressMode(MTL::SamplerAddressModeClampToEdge);
      m_Sampler = device->newSamplerState(samplerDesc);
      samplerDesc->release();
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(m_Resolution * m_Resolution * 8 * 6);
      KV_CORE_INFO("TextureCube: Generated Irradiance Map (Resolution: {0}x{0})", m_Resolution);
    }
    ~MetalTextureCube() override {
      if (m_Sampler) { m_Sampler->release(); m_Sampler = nullptr; }
      if (m_Texture) { m_Texture->release(); m_Texture = nullptr; }
      m_MemoryGuard.Reset(0);
    }
    void Bind(MTL::RenderCommandEncoder* encoder, uint32_t textureSlot, uint32_t samplerSlot) const override {
      KV_CORE_ASSERT(encoder != nullptr, "[KanVerse TextureCube] Command encoder cannot be null during bind!");
      encoder->setFragmentTexture(m_Texture, textureSlot);
      encoder->setFragmentSamplerState(m_Sampler, samplerSlot);
    }
    [[nodiscard]] uint32_t GetResolution() const noexcept override { return m_Resolution; }
    [[nodiscard]] MTL::Texture* GetNativeTexture() const noexcept override { return m_Texture; }
  private:
    uint32_t               m_Resolution{0};
    MTL::Texture*          m_Texture{nullptr};
    MTL::SamplerState*     m_Sampler{nullptr};
    ScopedMemoryAllocation m_MemoryGuard{};
  };
  std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> TextureCube::Create(const std::vector<std::string>& faces) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTextureCube>(faces);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> TextureCube::CreateProcedural(uint32_t resolution) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTextureCube>(ProceduralSkyParams{}, resolution);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> TextureCube::CreateProcedural(const ProceduralSkyParams& params, uint32_t resolution) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTextureCube>(params, resolution);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> TextureCube::CreateFromEquirectangular(const std::string& filepath, uint32_t resolution) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTextureCube>(filepath, resolution);
  }
  std::shared_ptr<TextureCube> TextureCube::CreateIrradianceMap(const std::shared_ptr<TextureCube>& sourceEnvMap, uint32_t resolution) {
    return std::make_shared<MetalTextureCube>(sourceEnvMap, resolution);
  }
} // namespace KanVerse
