// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/Bloom.metal
/*!
 * @file Bloom.metal
 * @brief Metal compute kernels for Dual-Kawase HDR bloom downsampling and upsampling.
 * @details Implements a multi-pass compute blur chain. The downsample stage extracts
 *          high-luminance pixels using a soft-knee threshold and downsamples with a
 *          13-tap anti-flicker kernel. The upsample stage expands lower-resolution mips
 *          using a 9-tap 3x3 tent filter with progressive additive accumulation.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
/*!
 * @struct BloomDownsampleUniforms
 * @brief Parameters for the compute downsampling pass (strictly 32 bytes).
 */
struct BloomDownsampleUniforms {
  float2 texelSize;   ///< Inverse dimensions of the source texture (8 bytes, offset 0).
  float  threshold;   ///< Minimum luminance cut-off value (4 bytes, offset 8).
  float  softKnee;    ///< Soft transition factor [0, 1] (4 bytes, offset 12).
  int    mipLevel;    ///< Current downsample hierarchy level (4 bytes, offset 16).
  float  padding[3];  ///< Array of 3 floats for 32-byte total alignment (12 bytes, offset 20).
};
/*!
 * @struct BloomUpsampleUniforms
 * @brief Parameters for the compute upsampling and additive blending pass (strictly 16 bytes).
 */
struct BloomUpsampleUniforms {
  float2 texelSize;     ///< Inverse dimensions of the lower-resolution mip texture (8 bytes).
  float  filterRadius;  ///< Blur sample radius expansion scalar (4 bytes).
  float  padding;       ///< Alignment padding (4 bytes).
};
/*!
 * @brief Computes relative perceived luminance using standard Rec. 709 coefficients.
 * @param color Linear RGB color vector.
 * @return Scalar luminance value.
 */
float Luminance(float3 color) {
  return dot(color, float3(0.2126f, 0.7152f, 0.0722f));
}
/*!
 * @brief Applies a quadratic soft-knee threshold curve to extract bright HDR highlights.
 * @param color Unfiltered source pixel color.
 * @param threshold Luminance threshold cut-off.
 * @param softKnee Knee sharpness factor.
 * @return Highlight color energy exceeding threshold.
 */
float3 Prefilter(float3 color, float threshold, float softKnee) {
  const float brightness = max(color.r, max(color.g, color.b));
  const float knee = threshold * softKnee;
  float soft = brightness - threshold + knee;
  soft = clamp(soft, 0.0f, 2.0f * knee);
  soft = (soft * soft) / (4.0f * max(knee, 1e-4f));
  float contribution = max(soft, brightness - threshold);
  contribution /= max(brightness, 1e-4f);
  return color * max(contribution, 0.0f);
}
// =============================================================================
// Compute Kernel: Downsample with 13-Tap Anti-Flicker Filter Pattern
// =============================================================================
/*!
 * @brief Downsamples the source texture by 2x using a 13-tap weighted box-and-cross kernel.
 * @param srcTexture Source input texture (sampled via bilinear clamp).
 * @param dstTexture Destination downscaled texture (half resolution).
 * @param uniforms Downsample parameters and thresholding settings.
 * @param linearSampler Bilinear clamp sampler.
 * @param gid 2D thread coordinate corresponding to target destination texel.
 */
kernel void bloom_downsample(texture2d<float, access::sample> srcTexture     [[texture(0)]],
                             texture2d<float, access::write>  dstTexture     [[texture(1)]],
                             constant BloomDownsampleUniforms& uniforms      [[buffer(0)]],
                             sampler                          linearSampler  [[sampler(0)]],
                             uint2                            gid            [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {
  // Boundary check against destination texture dimensions
  if (gid.x >= dstTexture.get_width() || gid.y >= dstTexture.get_height()) {
    return;
  }
  // Center UV coordinate in destination grid
  const float2 uv = (float2(gid) + 0.5f) * uniforms.texelSize;
  const float2 d  = uniforms.texelSize;
  // Sample 13 taps surrounding the central point to suppress sub-pixel flickering
  const float3 a = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-2.0f * d.x,  2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 b = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 0.0f,        2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 c = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 2.0f * d.x,  2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 d0 = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-2.0f * d.x,  0.0f)).rgb;
  const float3 e  = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv).rgb;
  const float3 f  = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 2.0f * d.x,  0.0f)).rgb;
  const float3 g = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-2.0f * d.x, -2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 h = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 0.0f,       -2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 i = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 2.0f * d.x, -2.0f * d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 j = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-d.x,  d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 k = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( d.x,  d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 l = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-d.x, -d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 m = srcTexture.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( d.x, -d.y)).rgb;
  // Weighted integration: center (12.5%), corners (12.5%), edges (25%), inner square (50%)
  float3 sampleSum = (e * 0.125f) +
  ((a + c + g + i) * 0.03125f) +
  ((b + d0 + f + h) * 0.0625f) +
  ((j + k + l + m) * 0.125f);
  // Apply bright pass prefilter strictly on the base mip (level 0)
  if (uniforms.mipLevel == 0) {
    sampleSum = Prefilter(sampleSum, uniforms.threshold, uniforms.softKnee);
  }
  dstTexture.write(float4(sampleSum, 1.0f), gid);
}
// =============================================================================
// Compute Kernel: Upsample with 9-Tap 3x3 Tent Filter & Additive Accumulation
// =============================================================================
/*!
 * @brief Upsamples a lower-resolution mip, blurs via a 3x3 tent filter, and adds to the higher mip.
 * @param srcLowerMip Coarser mip level to upsample.
 * @param srcHigherMip Finer mip level to blend into.
 * @param dstTexture Output destination holding the accumulated blur.
 * @param uniforms Filter radius and texel size configuration.
 * @param linearSampler Bilinear clamp sampler.
 * @param gid 2D thread coordinate in the target higher-resolution grid.
 */
kernel void bloom_upsample(texture2d<float, access::sample> srcLowerMip   [[texture(0)]],
                           texture2d<float, access::sample> srcHigherMip  [[texture(1)]],
                           texture2d<float, access::write>  dstTexture    [[texture(2)]],
                           constant BloomUpsampleUniforms&  uniforms      [[buffer(0)]],
                           sampler                          linearSampler [[sampler(0)]],
                           uint2                            gid           [[thread_position_in_grid]]) {
  // Boundary check against destination texture dimensions
  if (gid.x >= dstTexture.get_width() || gid.y >= dstTexture.get_height()) {
    return;
  }
  const float2 uv = (float2(gid) + 0.5f) * uniforms.texelSize;
  const float2 d  = uniforms.texelSize * uniforms.filterRadius;
  // 9-tap 3x3 tent filter sampling pattern
  const float3 a = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-d.x,  d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 b = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 0.0f, d.y)).rgb * 2.0f;
  const float3 c = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( d.x,  d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 d0 = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-d.x,  0.0f)).rgb * 2.0f;
  const float3 e  = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv).rgb * 4.0f;
  const float3 f  = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( d.x,  0.0f)).rgb * 2.0f;
  const float3 g = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2(-d.x, -d.y)).rgb;
  const float3 h = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( 0.0f, -d.y)).rgb * 2.0f;
  const float3 i = srcLowerMip.sample(linearSampler, uv + float2( d.x, -d.y)).rgb;
  // Normalization factor: 1 + 2 + 1 + 2 + 4 + 2 + 1 + 2 + 1 = 16
  const float3 upsampled = (a + b + c + d0 + e + f + g + h + i) * (1.0f / 16.0f);
  // Additive accumulation: blend upsampled blur with current mip's details
  const float3 current = srcHigherMip.sample(linearSampler, uv).rgb;
  const float3 accumulated = current + upsampled;
  dstTexture.write(float4(accumulated, 1.0f), gid);
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/DebrisSimulation.metal

// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/Equirectangular.metal
/*!
 * @file Equirectangular.metal
 * @brief High-throughput depth-only vertex shader for directional shadow map rasterization.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
// Helper function to map a 3D unit direction vector to a 2D Spherical (Equirectangular) UV coordinate
float2 SampleSphericalMap(float3 v) {
  // Add clamp to prevent NaN at absolute poles due to float imprecision
  float2 uv = float2(atan2(v.z, v.x), asin(clamp(v.y, -1.0f, 1.0f)));
  // Scale by inverse of 2*PI and PI to normalize to [0.0, 1.0]
  uv *= float2(0.15915494309f, 0.31830988618f);
  uv += 0.5f;
  return uv;
}
kernel void equirectangular_to_cubemap(
                                       texture2d<float, access::sample>      equirectangularTexture [[texture(0)]],
                                       texture2d_array<float, access::write> cubemapTexture         [[texture(1)]],
                                       uint3                                 tid                    [[thread_position_in_grid]])
{
  uint2 size = uint2(cubemapTexture.get_width(), cubemapTexture.get_height());
  if (tid.x >= size.x || tid.y >= size.y || tid.z >= 6) {
    return;
  }
  float2 uv = float2(tid.xy) / float2(size.x - 1, size.y - 1);
  uv = uv * 2.0f - 1.0f;
  uv.y = -uv.y;
  float3 dir;
  switch(tid.z) {
    case 0: dir = float3( 1.0f,  uv.y, -uv.x); break; // +X (Right)
    case 1: dir = float3(-1.0f,  uv.y,  uv.x); break; // -X (Left)
    case 2: dir = float3( uv.x,  1.0f, -uv.y); break; // +Y (Top)
    case 3: dir = float3( uv.x, -1.0f,  uv.y); break; // -Y (Bottom)
    case 4: dir = float3( uv.x,  uv.y,  1.0f); break; // +Z (Front)
    case 5: dir = float3(-uv.x,  uv.y, -1.0f); break; // -Z (Back)
  }
  dir = normalize(dir);
  // --- REPLACE YOUR OLD SAMPLER WITH THIS ONE HERE ---
  constexpr sampler linearSampler(
                                  coord::normalized,
                                  min_filter::linear,
                                  mag_filter::linear,
                                  mip_filter::none,
                                  s_address::repeat,
                                  t_address::clamp_to_edge
                                  );
  float4 color = equirectangularTexture.sample(linearSampler, SampleSphericalMap(dir));
  cubemapTexture.write(color, tid.xy, tid.z);
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/Irradiance.metal
/*!
 * @file Irradiance.metal
 * @brief High-throughput depth-only vertex shader for directional shadow map rasterization.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
constant float PI = 3.14159265359f;
kernel void irradiance_convolution(
                                   texturecube<float, access::sample>    envMap        [[texture(0)]],
                                   texture2d_array<float, access::write> irradianceMap [[texture(1)]],
                                   uint3                                 tid           [[thread_position_in_grid]])
{
  uint2 size = uint2(irradianceMap.get_width(), irradianceMap.get_height());
  if (tid.x >= size.x || tid.y >= size.y || tid.z >= 6) return;
  // Map thread ID to [-1.0, 1.0] for the current face
  float2 uv = float2(tid.xy) / float2(size.x - 1, size.y - 1);
  uv = uv * 2.0f - 1.0f;
  uv.y = -uv.y;
  float3 N;
  switch(tid.z) {
    case 0: N = float3( 1.0f,  uv.y, -uv.x); break; // +X
    case 1: N = float3(-1.0f,  uv.y,  uv.x); break; // -X
    case 2: N = float3( uv.x,  1.0f, -uv.y); break; // +Y
    case 3: N = float3( uv.x, -1.0f,  uv.y); break; // -Y
    case 4: N = float3( uv.x,  uv.y,  1.0f); break; // +Z
    case 5: N = float3(-uv.x,  uv.y, -1.0f); break; // -Z
  }
  N = normalize(N);
  // Tangent space calculation
  float3 up    = float3(0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f);
  float3 right = normalize(cross(up, N));
  up           = normalize(cross(N, right));
  float3 irradiance = float3(0.0f);
  float sampleDelta = 0.025f;
  float nrSamples = 0.0f;
  constexpr sampler linearSampler(min_filter::linear, mag_filter::linear, mip_filter::linear);
  // Integrate over the hemisphere
  for(float phi = 0.0f; phi < 2.0f * PI; phi += sampleDelta) {
    for(float theta = 0.0f; theta < 0.5f * PI; theta += sampleDelta) {
      // Spherical to cartesian (in tangent space)
      float3 tangentSample = float3(sin(theta) * cos(phi),  sin(theta) * sin(phi), cos(theta));
      // Tangent space to world
      float3 sampleVec = tangentSample.x * right + tangentSample.y * up + tangentSample.z * N;
      irradiance += envMap.sample(linearSampler, sampleVec).rgb * cos(theta) * sin(theta);
      nrSamples++;
    }
  }
  irradiance = PI * irradiance * (1.0f / nrSamples);
  // FIX: Tone down and clamp extreme HDR hot-spots so they don't blow out into white splotches
  irradiance = irradiance / (irradiance + 1.0f); // Simple Reinhard tone operator for ambient term
  // Write out the smooth, balanced irradiance
  irradianceMap.write(float4(irradiance, 1.0f), tid.xy, tid.z);
}

// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/ShadowDepth.metal
/*!
 * @file ShadowDepth.metal
 * @brief High-throughput depth-only vertex shader for directional shadow map rasterization.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
struct VertexInput {
  float3 position [[attribute(0)]];
};
struct ShadowUniforms {
  float4x4 lightViewProjectionMatrix;
};
struct InstanceData {
  float4x4 modelMatrix;
  float4x4 normalMatrix;
};
struct ShadowVertexOutput {
  float4 clipPosition [[position]];
};
vertex ShadowVertexOutput shadow_vertex_main(VertexInput             in            [[stage_in]],
                                             constant ShadowUniforms& uniforms      [[buffer(1)]],
                                             constant InstanceData*  instances     [[buffer(3)]],
                                             uint                    instanceID    [[instance_id]]) {
  ShadowVertexOutput out;
  constant InstanceData& instance = instances[instanceID];
  float4 worldPosition = instance.modelMatrix * float4(in.position, 1.0);
  out.clipPosition     = uniforms.lightViewProjectionMatrix * worldPosition;
  return out;
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/Skybox.metal
/*!
 * @file Skybox.metal
 * @brief High-throughput depth-only vertex shader for directional shadow map rasterization.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
struct SkyboxVertexOut {
  float4 position [[position]];
  float3 viewDir;
};
// We pass the inverse matrices to un-project the screen back into the 3D world
struct SkyboxUniforms {
  float4x4 inverseView;
  float4x4 inverseProjection;
};
vertex SkyboxVertexOut skybox_vertex(uint vertexID [[vertex_id]],
                                     constant SkyboxUniforms& uniforms [[buffer(0)]]) {
  // Generate a fullscreen triangle without needing a vertex buffer
  float2 uv = float2((vertexID << 1) & 2, vertexID & 2);
  float4 clipPos = float4(uv * 2.0f - 1.0f, 1.0f, 1.0f); // Z = 1.0 pushes it to the absolute background
  SkyboxVertexOut out;
  out.position = clipPos;
  // Un-project from 2D screen space to 3D view space
  float4 viewPos = uniforms.inverseProjection * clipPos;
  viewPos /= viewPos.w;
  // Convert to world space direction (w = 0.0 ignores camera translation so the skybox feels infinitely far away)
  out.viewDir = (uniforms.inverseView * float4(viewPos.xyz, 0.0f)).xyz;
  // Invert the Y axis to match Metal's texture coordinate system
  out.viewDir.y = -out.viewDir.y;
  return out;
}
fragment float4 skybox_fragment(SkyboxVertexOut in [[stage_in]],
                                texturecube<float> envMap [[texture(0)]],
                                sampler envSampler [[sampler(0)]]) {
  // Sample the cubemap using the calculated 3D ray direction
  float3 dir = normalize(in.viewDir);
  return envMap.sample(envSampler, dir);
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/TexturedMaterial.metal
/*!
 * @file TexturedMaterial.metal
 * @brief Cook-Torrance PBR shader with seamless reflection sampling, smooth shadow frustum fade, and point lights.
 * @copyright KanVerse Engine
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
// Mathematical constant Pi for energy-conserving BRDF normalization
constant float PI = 3.14159265358979323846f;
// Maximum number of dynamic point lights evaluated per fragment (matches Renderer::kMaxPointLights in C++)
constant uint kMaxPointLights = 64;
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * 1. VERTEX STAGE INPUT & OUTPUT STRUCTURES
 * ============================================================================
 * VertexInput maps directly to the MTL::VertexDescriptor bound at [[buffer(0)]].
 * Note that in Metal, [[stage_in]] unpacks attributes from the vertex buffer
 * using the offsets defined in Renderer::GetStandardMeshLayout().
 */
struct VertexInput {
  float3 position  [[attribute(0)]]; // Offset 0  (16 bytes in C++ simd::float3)
  float3 normal    [[attribute(1)]]; // Offset 16 (16 bytes in C++ simd::float3)
  float4 color     [[attribute(2)]]; // Offset 32 (16 bytes in C++ simd::float4)
  float2 texCoord  [[attribute(3)]]; // Offset 48 (8 bytes in C++ simd::float2)
};
/*
 * Interpolated payload passed from the Vertex Shader to the Rasterizer and Fragment Shader.
 */
struct VertexOutput {
  float4 position [[position]]; // Homogeneous clip-space position (x, y, z, w) for rasterization
  float3 worldPosition;         // Fragment position in 3D world space (used for lighting & derivatives)
  float3 worldNormal;           // Geometric surface normal in 3D world space
  float4 color;                 // Per-vertex color multiplier
  float2 texCoord;              // Base 2D UV coordinates [0, 1]
  float4 shadowPosition;        // Fragment position projected into the Sun's orthographic light space
};
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * 2. CONSTANT BUFFER STRUCTURES (MUST MATCH C++ MEMORY LAYOUT BIT-FOR-BIT)
 * ============================================================================
 */
/*
 * Matches KanVerse::GPUPointLight (48 bytes total, 16-byte aligned).
 * Packs scalars into float4s to prevent hidden SIMD padding discrepancies.
 */
struct GPUPointLight {
  float4 positionAndRadius;  // .xyz = World Position, .w = Cutoff Radius in meters
  float4 colorAndIntensity;  // .rgb = Linear RGB Color, .a = Luminous Intensity multiplier
  float4 attenuation;        // .x = Constant, .y = Linear, .z = Quadratic falloff, .w = Unused
};
/*
 * Bound at [[buffer(1)]] in both Vertex and Fragment stages.
 * Matches KanVerse::Renderer::GPUUniforms in Renderer.hpp (256-byte aligned).
 * Note: In Metal Shading Language, `float3` occupies 16 bytes (same as C++ `simd::float3`).
 */
struct GPUUniforms {
  float4x4      viewProjectionMatrix;             // Camera Projection * View matrix (64 bytes, offset 0)
  float4x4      lightSpaceMatrix;                 // Sun Orthographic Proj * View matrix (64 bytes, offset 64)
  float3        cameraPosition;                   // World-space eye coordinates (16 bytes, offset 128)
  float3        lightDirection;                   // Normalized direction the sun rays travel (16 bytes, offset 144)
  float4        directionalColor;                 // Sun RGB color * intensity (16 bytes, offset 160)
  float4        ambientColor;                     // Sky ambient RGB * intensity (16 bytes, offset 176)
  uint          pointLightCount;                  // Active point lights in the scene [0..64] (4 bytes, offset 192)
  uint          padding[3];                       // 12-byte padding to align pointLights array to 16-byte boundary (offset 196)
  GPUPointLight pointLights[kMaxPointLights];     // Array of 64 point lights (64 * 48 = 3072 bytes, offset 208)
};
/*
 * Bound at [[buffer(3)]] in the Vertex stage.
 * Matches KanVerse::InstanceData in Renderer.hpp (128 bytes per instance).
 */
struct InstanceData {
  float4x4 modelMatrix;  // Local-to-World transformation matrix (64 bytes)
  float4x4 normalMatrix; // Transpose(Inverse(modelMatrix)) to transform normals under non-uniform scale (64 bytes)
};
/*
 * Bound at [[buffer(2)]] in the Fragment stage.
 * Matches KanVerse::MaterialUniforms in Material.hpp (256-byte aligned).
 */
struct MaterialUniforms {
  float4   baseColor;        // RGBA Albedo tint (16 bytes, offset 0)
  float4   specularColor;    // Specular reflectance tint (16 bytes, offset 16)
  float4   emissiveColor;    // HDR self-illumination radiance for bloom (16 bytes, offset 32)
  float3   tilingFactor;     // World-axis UV tiling scale (16 bytes in MSL float3, offset 48)
  float    roughness;        // Microfacet roughness scalar [0.05..1.0] (4 bytes, offset 64)
  float    metallic;         // Metalness scalar [0.0..1.0] (4 bytes, offset 68)
  int32_t  hasAlbedoMap;     // 1 if [[texture(0)]] has a valid texture, 0 if using fallback (4 bytes, offset 72)
  int32_t  hasNormalMap;     // 1 if [[texture(1)]] has a valid normal map (4 bytes, offset 76)
  int32_t  hasRoughnessMap;  // 1 if [[texture(2)]] has a valid roughness map (4 bytes, offset 80)
  int32_t  hasMetallicMap;   // 1 if [[texture(3)]] has a valid metallic map (4 bytes, offset 84)
  int32_t  hasAOMap;         // 1 if [[texture(4)]] has a valid ambient occlusion map (4 bytes, offset 88)
  int32_t  hasHeightMap;     // 1 if [[texture(5)]] has a valid height map (4 bytes, offset 92)
  float    padding;          // Trailing alignment padding (4 bytes, offset 96)
};
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * 3. COOK-TORRANCE MICROFACET BRDF MATHEMATICAL FUNCTIONS
 * ============================================================================
 * The Cook-Torrance specular BRDF is defined as:
 *
 *               D(N, H, a) * G(N, V, L, k) * F(H, V, F0)
 *   f_spec = ----------------------------------------------
 *                    4 * dot(N, V) * dot(N, L)
 */
/*
 * Trowbridge-Reitz GGX Normal Distribution Function (D):
 * Computes the statistical fraction of microscopic surface facets that are aligned
 * with the halfway vector H. Lower roughness creates a tiny, blindingly bright highlight;
 * higher roughness spreads microfacets wide for a chalky appearance.
 */
inline float DistributionGGX(float3 N, float3 H, float roughness) {
  float a      = roughness * roughness; // Disney/Epic perceptual roughness remapping (alpha = roughness^2)
  float a2     = a * a;
  float NdotH  = max(dot(N, H), 0.0f);
  float NdotH2 = NdotH * NdotH;
  float num   = a2;
  float denom = (NdotH2 * (a2 - 1.0f) + 1.0f);
  denom       = PI * denom * denom;
  return num / max(denom, 1e-4f); // Guard against division by zero when roughness approaches 0
}
/*
 * Schlick-GGX Geometry Shadowing Helper:
 * Approximates how microfacets self-occlude or overshadow one another along a single vector.
 */
inline float GeometrySchlickGGX(float NdotV, float roughness) {
  // Direct lighting remapping: k = (roughness + 1)^2 / 8
  float r = (roughness + 1.0f);
  float k = (r * r) / 8.0f;
  float num   = NdotV;
  float denom = NdotV * (1.0f - k) + k;
  return num / max(denom, 1e-4f);
}
/*
 * Smith's Method for Geometry Attenuation (G):
 * Combines both view-direction microfacet masking (NdotV) and light-direction
 * microfacet shadowing (NdotL).
 */
inline float GeometrySmith(float3 N, float3 V, float3 L, float roughness) {
  float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0f);
  float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0f);
  float ggx2  = GeometrySchlickGGX(NdotV, roughness);
  float ggx1  = GeometrySchlickGGX(NdotL, roughness);
  return ggx1 * ggx2;
}
/*
 * Schlick's Approximation for the Fresnel Equation (F):
 * Computes the ratio of light that reflects at a given viewing angle (cosTheta).
 * At grazing angles (cosTheta -> 0), all surfaces approach 100% specular reflection.
 * F0 is the base reflectivity at normal incidence (0.04 for dielectrics, albedo for metals).
 */
inline float3 FresnelSchlick(float cosTheta, float3 F0) {
  return F0 + (1.0f - F0) * pow(clamp(1.0f - cosTheta, 0.0f, 1.0f), 5.0f);
}
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * 4. VERTEX SHADER STAGE
 * ============================================================================
 * Executes once per vertex per instance. Uses hardware instancing ([[instance_id]])
 * to draw thousands of walls, floor tiles, and debris shards in a single draw call.
 */
vertex VertexOutput vertex_main(VertexInput             in         [[stage_in]],
                                constant GPUUniforms&   uniforms   [[buffer(1)]],
                                constant InstanceData*  instances  [[buffer(3)]],
                                uint                    instanceID [[instance_id]]) {
  VertexOutput out;
  // 1. Fetch this specific instance's transformation matrices from [[buffer(3)]]
  constant InstanceData& instance = instances[instanceID];
  // 2. Transform local vertex position into 3D World Space, then into Clip Space
  float4 worldPosition = instance.modelMatrix * float4(in.position, 1.0f);
  out.worldPosition    = worldPosition.xyz;
  out.position         = uniforms.viewProjectionMatrix * worldPosition;
  // 3. Transform local surface normal using the Inverse-Transpose normalMatrix
  //    (w = 0.0f ignores translation; normalMatrix prevents skewing on non-uniformly scaled walls)
  out.worldNormal = (instance.normalMatrix * float4(in.normal, 0.0f)).xyz;
  out.color       = in.color;
  out.texCoord    = in.texCoord;
  // 4. Normal-Offset Shadow Bias:
  //    Push the shadow lookup position 3.5 cm outward along the true geometric normal.
  //    This eliminates "shadow acne" staircase artifacts on flat ground and steep walls
  //    without causing "peter-panning" (detached shadows).
  float3 worldGeomNormal = normalize(out.worldNormal);
  float4 shadowWorldPos  = float4(worldPosition.xyz + worldGeomNormal * 0.035f, 1.0f);
  out.shadowPosition     = uniforms.lightSpaceMatrix * shadowWorldPos;
  return out;
}
/*
 * ============================================================================
 * 5. FRAGMENT SHADER STAGE
 * ============================================================================
 * Evaluates full Cook-Torrance PBR + PCF Shadows + 64 Point Lights + IBL per pixel.
 */
fragment float4 fragment_main(VertexOutput              in            [[stage_in]],
                              constant GPUUniforms&     uniforms      [[buffer(1)]],
                              constant MaterialUniforms& material     [[buffer(2)]],
                              texture2d<float>          albedoTex     [[texture(0)]],
                              texture2d<float>          normalTex     [[texture(1)]],
                              texture2d<float>          roughnessTex  [[texture(2)]],
                              texture2d<float>          metallicTex   [[texture(3)]],
                              texture2d<float>          aoTex         [[texture(4)]],
                              texture2d<float>          heightTex     [[texture(5)]],
                              depth2d<float>            shadowMap     [[texture(6)]],
                              texturecube<float>        envMap        [[texture(7)]],
                              texturecube<float>        irradianceMap [[texture(8)]],
                              sampler                   texSampler    [[sampler(0)]],
                              sampler                   shadowSampler [[sampler(1)]]) {
  // Re-normalize interpolated geometric normal across the triangle face
  float3 N_geom = normalize(in.worldNormal);
  float3 N      = N_geom;
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * Step A: Box-Aligned Triplanar UV Tiling
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * Scales the [0, 1] UVs based on which world axis the face normal is most aligned with:
   * - Front/Back walls (|N.z| > 0.5): tile by (Width X, Height Y)
   * - Left/Right walls (|N.x| > 0.5): tile by (Depth Z, Height Y)
   * - Floors/Ceilings  (|N.y| > 0.5): tile by (Width X, Depth Z)
   */
  float2 tiledUV = in.texCoord;
  if (abs(N_geom.z) > 0.5f) {
    tiledUV *= material.tilingFactor.xy;
  } else if (abs(N_geom.x) > 0.5f) {
    tiledUV *= material.tilingFactor.zy;
  } else {
    tiledUV *= material.tilingFactor.xz;
  }
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * Step B: Sample Albedo, Normal, Roughness, Metallic, and AO Maps
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   */
  float3 texColor = float3(1.0f);
  if (material.hasAlbedoMap != 0) {
    // albedoTex is bound as RGBA8Unorm_sRGB, so hardware automatically converts sRGB -> Linear
    texColor = albedoTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).rgb;
  }
  float3 albedo = in.color.rgb * material.baseColor.rgb * texColor;
  /*
   * Screen-Space Derivative TBN Normal Mapping (Cotangent Frame):
   * Instead of storing extra float3 Tangent and Bitangent vectors in every Vertex
   * (which would bloat Vertex size from 64B to 96B), we compute the tangent basis (T, B, N)
   * on the fly using hardware 2x2 pixel quad partial derivatives (dfdx, dfdy).
   */
  if (material.hasNormalMap != 0) {
    // Unpack tangent-space normal from [0, 1] texture range to [-1, 1] vector range
    float3 normalPixel = normalTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).rgb;
    float3 localNormal = normalize(normalPixel * 2.0f - 1.0f);
    // Scale XY perturbation slightly (0.65) so extreme grazing angles on flat ground never invert NdotV
    localNormal.xy *= 0.65f;
    localNormal = normalize(localNormal);
    // Compute rate of change of 3D world position and 2D UVs across the 2x2 pixel quad
    float3 Q1     = dfdx(in.worldPosition);
    float3 Q2     = dfdy(in.worldPosition);
    float2 st1    = dfdx(tiledUV);
    float2 st2    = dfdy(tiledUV);
    float  det    = st1.x * st2.y - st2.x * st1.y;
    float  invDet = (det < 0.0f) ? -1.0f : 1.0f;
    float3 T_raw  = (Q1 * st2.y - Q2 * st1.y) * invDet;
    // Gram-Schmidt orthogonalization: project T perpendicular to N_geom
    float3 T_ortho = T_raw - N_geom * dot(N_geom, T_raw);
    float  tLenSq  = dot(T_ortho, T_ortho);
    if (tLenSq > 1e-8f) {
      float3 T     = T_ortho * rsqrt(tLenSq);
      float3 B     = cross(N_geom, T);
      float3x3 TBN = float3x3(T, B, N_geom);
      N            = normalize(TBN * localNormal);
    }
  }
  // Sample Roughness (clamped to >= 0.05 so GGX denominator never explodes to infinity)
  float roughness = material.roughness;
  if (material.hasRoughnessMap != 0) {
    roughness *= roughnessTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).r;
  }
  roughness = clamp(roughness, 0.05f, 1.0f);
  // Sample Metallic [0 = Dielectric (plastic/stone/wood), 1 = Pure Conductor (gold/iron/steel)]
  float metallic = material.metallic;
  if (material.hasMetallicMap != 0) {
    metallic *= metallicTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).r;
  }
  metallic = clamp(metallic, 0.0f, 1.0f);
  // Sample Ambient Occlusion (darkens crevices and corners)
  float ao = 1.0f;
  if (material.hasAOMap != 0) {
    ao = aoTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).r;
  }
  // View vector V pointing from surface fragment toward the camera eye
  float3 V  = normalize(uniforms.cameraPosition - in.worldPosition);
  // Base reflectivity F0: non-metals reflect 4% (0.04) monochrome; metals tint reflections with their albedo
  float3 F0 = mix(float3(0.04f), albedo, metallic);
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * 1. Directional Sun/Moon Light + 3x3 PCF Shadow Mapping
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   */
  float3 L = normalize(-uniforms.lightDirection); // Vector pointing toward the sun
  float3 H = normalize(V + L);                    // Halfway vector between View and Light
  // Evaluate Cook-Torrance BRDF terms for the Sun
  float  NDF = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
  float  G   = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
  float3 F   = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0f), F0);
  float3 numerator   = NDF * G * F;
  float  denominator = 4.0f * max(dot(N, V), 0.0f) * max(dot(N, L), 0.0f) + 1e-4f;
  float3 specular    = min(numerator / denominator, float3(1.5f)); // Clamp extreme specular fireflies
  // Energy Conservation: Diffuse light (kD) is whatever energy was not reflected as Specular (kS = F).
  // Pure metals (metallic = 1.0) have zero diffuse scattering (kD = 0).
  float3 kS    = F;
  float3 kD    = (float3(1.0f) - kS) * (1.0f - metallic);
  float  NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0f);
  // Perspective divide to convert light-space position to Normalized Device Coordinates (NDC)
  float  shadowFactor = 1.0f;
  float3 projCoords   = in.shadowPosition.xyz / in.shadowPosition.w;
  // Remap X and Y from NDC [-1, 1] to Metal texture UV coordinates [0, 1].
  // Note: Y is negated (-0.5f) because Metal texture origin (0,0) is Top-Left, whereas NDC +Y is Up.
  projCoords.xy = projCoords.xy * float2(0.5f, -0.5f) + float2(0.5f, 0.5f);
  // Check if fragment lies inside the shadow map frustum (with a 1% inset margin for 3x3 PCF taps)
  if (projCoords.x >= 0.01f && projCoords.x <= 0.99f &&
      projCoords.y >= 0.01f && projCoords.y <= 0.99f &&
      projCoords.z >= 0.0f  && projCoords.z <= 1.0f) {
    float currentDepth = projCoords.z;
    // Slope-scaled depth bias: steeper surfaces relative to the sun get slightly more bias
    float bias      = max(0.0020f * (1.0f - max(dot(N_geom, L), 0.0f)), 0.0008f);
    float shadowSum = 0.0f;
    float2 texelSize = 1.0f / float2(shadowMap.get_width(), shadowMap.get_height());
    // 3x3 Percentage-Closer Filtering (PCF): average 9 depth comparisons for soft shadow penumbras
    for (int x = -1; x <= 1; ++x) {
      for (int y = -1; y <= 1; ++y) {
        float pcfDepth = shadowMap.sample(shadowSampler, projCoords.xy + float2(x, y) * texelSize);
        shadowSum += (currentDepth - bias > pcfDepth) ? 0.0f : 1.0f;
      }
    }
    float rawShadow = shadowSum / 9.0f;
    // Smoothly fade shadow out over the outer 8% border of the ortho box so no hard box edge is visible
    float2 edgeDist = abs(projCoords.xy * 2.0f - 1.0f);
    float  edgeFade = smoothstep(0.92f, 1.0f, max(edgeDist.x, edgeDist.y));
    shadowFactor    = mix(rawShadow, 1.0f, edgeFade);
  } else {
    // Outside shadow frustum: smoothly blend to 1.0 (fully lit)
    float2 edgeDist = abs(projCoords.xy * 2.0f - 1.0f);
    float  outFade  = smoothstep(1.0f, 1.05f, max(edgeDist.x, edgeDist.y));
    shadowFactor    = mix(0.0f, 1.0f, outFade);
  }
  float3 radiance        = uniforms.directionalColor.rgb;
  float  effectiveShadow = mix(0.12f, 1.0f, shadowFactor); // Keep a subtle 12% bounce light floor in deep shadows
  float3 directLighting  = (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL * effectiveShadow;
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * 2. Dynamic Point Lights (Wall Sconces, Ceiling Lights & Street Lamps)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   */
  float3 pointLighting = float3(0.0f);
  uint   lightCount    = min(uniforms.pointLightCount, kMaxPointLights);
  for (uint i = 0; i < lightCount; ++i) {
    float3 lightPos = uniforms.pointLights[i].positionAndRadius.xyz;
    float  radius   = max(uniforms.pointLights[i].positionAndRadius.w, 1.0f);
    float3 lColor   = uniforms.pointLights[i].colorAndIntensity.rgb;
    float  lInten   = clamp(uniforms.pointLights[i].colorAndIntensity.w, 0.0f, 12.0f);
    float3 atten    = max(uniforms.pointLights[i].attenuation.xyz, float3(1.0f, 0.22f, 0.045f));
    float3 toLight  = lightPos - in.worldPosition;
    float  distSq   = dot(toLight, toLight);
    float  radiusSq = radius * radius;
    // Early-out branch: skip BRDF evaluation if fragment is outside the point light's cutoff sphere
    if (distSq < radiusSq) {
      float  dist = sqrt(max(distSq, 1e-4f));
      float3 Lp   = toLight / dist;
      float3 Hp   = normalize(V + Lp);
      // Blend perturbed normal with geometric normal so normal maps don't create harsh black self-shadow seams
      float NdotL_geom = dot(N_geom, Lp);
      float NdotL_pert = dot(N, Lp);
      float NdotL_p    = max(mix(NdotL_pert, NdotL_geom, 0.35f), 0.0f);
      if (NdotL_p > 0.0f) {
        // Unreal/Frostbite smooth windowing function: forces attenuation to hit 0.0 smoothly at dist == radius
        float distRatio2 = distSq / radiusSq;
        float window     = saturate(1.0f - distRatio2 * distRatio2);
        window          *= window;
        // Physical Constant-Linear-Quadratic distance falloff
        float denomAtten  = atten.x + atten.y * dist + atten.z * distSq;
        float attenuation = (lInten * window) / denomAtten;
        // Point light Cook-Torrance BRDF
        float  NDF_p  = DistributionGGX(N, Hp, roughness);
        float  G_p    = GeometrySmith(N, V, Lp, roughness);
        float3 F_p    = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(Hp, V), 0.0f), F0);
        float3 spec_p = min((NDF_p * G_p * F_p) /
                            (4.0f * max(dot(N, V), 0.0f) * max(NdotL_p, 0.1f) + 1e-4f),
                            float3(0.8f));
        float3 kD_p   = (float3(1.0f) - F_p) * (1.0f - metallic);
        pointLighting += (kD_p * albedo / PI + spec_p) * lColor * attenuation * NdotL_p;
      }
    }
  }
  pointLighting = min(pointLighting, float3(1.05f)); // Prevent overlapping sconces from blowing out white
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * 3. Horizon-Stabilized Image-Based Lighting (Diffuse & Specular IBL)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   */
  constexpr sampler cubeSampler(coord::normalized, filter::linear, address::clamp_to_edge);
  // Diffuse IBL: sample pre-convolved irradiance cubemap using inverted Y to match Metal cubemap coordinates
  float3 sampleN       = normalize(mix(N, N_geom, 0.70f));
  sampleN.y            = -sampleN.y;
  float3 rawIrradiance = irradianceMap.sample(cubeSampler, sampleN).rgb;
  rawIrradiance        = rawIrradiance / (rawIrradiance + 1.0f); // Reinhard compression
  float  skyOcclusion = mix(0.25f, 1.0f, shadowFactor);
  float3 ambientTerm  = mix(uniforms.ambientColor.rgb, rawIrradiance * uniforms.ambientColor.rgb * 2.2f, 0.5f) * skyOcclusion;
  float3 diffuseIBL   = kD * albedo * ambientTerm * ao;
  // Specular IBL: reflect view ray about surface normal and sample mipmapped environment cubemap.
  // Smooth surfaces (roughness -> 0) sample sharp Mip 0; rough surfaces sample blurry higher mips.
  float3 N_refl  = normalize(mix(N, N_geom, 0.75f));
  float3 R       = reflect(-V, N_refl);
  float3 sampleR = R;
  sampleR.y      = -abs(R.y) - 0.05f; // Stabilize horizon lookup to prevent a moving seam line on flat ground
  sampleR        = normalize(sampleR);
  float  maxMipLevel = float(max(envMap.get_num_mip_levels(), 1u) - 1u);
  float  sampleMip   = roughness * maxMipLevel;
  float3 envColor    = envMap.sample(cubeSampler, sampleR, level(sampleMip)).rgb;
  envColor           = (envColor / (envColor + 1.0f)) * clamp(length(uniforms.ambientColor.rgb), 0.05f, 0.55f) * skyOcclusion;
  // Roughness-aware Fresnel for IBL: prevents rough stone/grass from having mirror-like grazing edges
  float  NdotV       = max(dot(N_geom, V), 0.0f);
  float3 F_ibl       = F0 + (max(float3(1.0f - roughness), F0) - F0) * pow(clamp(1.0f - NdotV, 0.0f, 1.0f), 5.0f);
  float3 specularIBL = envColor * F_ibl * (1.0f - roughness) * ao;
  /*
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   * 4. Final Radiance Accumulation & Alpha Output
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------------
   */
  float3 color = directLighting + pointLighting + diffuseIBL + specularIBL + material.emissiveColor.rgb;
  float finalAlpha = material.baseColor.a;
  if (material.hasAlbedoMap != 0) {
    finalAlpha = albedoTex.sample(texSampler, tiledUV).a * material.baseColor.a;
  }
  // Output linear HDR RGBA16Float color (Tonemapping + Compute Bloom will resolve this in EndScene)
  return float4(color, finalAlpha);
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Shaders/Tonemap.metal
/*!
 * @file Tonemap.metal
 * @brief Fullscreen ACES tonemapping and HDR resolve pass with Bloom composite.
 * @details Generates an oversized viewport-covering triangle in clip space without
 *          vertex buffers, composites the blurred compute bloom texture with the HDR
 *          scene buffer, and maps the result to SDR via the ACES filmic curve.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#include <metal_stdlib>
using namespace metal;
/*!
 * @struct TonemapVertexOutput
 * @brief Interpolated vertex stage payload passed into the tonemapping fragment shader.
 */
struct TonemapVertexOutput {
  float4 position [[position]]; ///< Clip-space coordinate covering NDC [-1, 1].
  float2 texCoord;             ///< UV texture coordinate covering [0, 1].
};
/*!
 * @struct TonemapUniforms
 * @brief Per-frame exposure and bloom composite parameters.
 */
struct TonemapUniforms {
  float bloomIntensity; ///< Multiplier scaling the additive bloom contribution.
  float exposure;       ///< Linear exposure multiplier applied to the scene color.
  float2 padding;       ///< 16-byte alignment padding.
};
/*!
 * @brief Synthesizes an oversized viewport-covering triangle using vertex ID indices.
 * @param vertexID Hardware generated vertex identifier (0, 1, 2).
 * @return TonemapVertexOutput with normalized coordinates.
 */
vertex TonemapVertexOutput tonemap_vertex(uint vertexID [[vertex_id]]) {
  TonemapVertexOutput out;
  // Three vertices: (-1, -1), (3, -1), (-1, 3) covering the full viewport
  out.texCoord = float2((vertexID << 1) & 2, vertexID & 2);
  out.position = float4(out.texCoord * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
  out.texCoord.y = 1.0 - out.texCoord.y; // Correct Y orientation for Metal textures
  return out;
}
/*!
 * @brief Evaluates the Narkowicz 2015 ACES Filmic curve fit for natural highlight compression.
 * @param x Linear radiant color vector.
 * @return Tonemapped linear color clamped within [0, 1].
 */
float3 ACESFilm(float3 x) {
  constexpr float a = 2.51f;
  constexpr float b = 0.03f;
  constexpr float c = 2.43f;
  constexpr float d = 0.59f;
  constexpr float e = 0.14f;
  return clamp((x * (a * x + b)) / (x * (c * x + d) + e), 0.0f, 1.0f);
}
/*!
 * @brief Resolves HDR scene radiance and bloom into tonemapped SDR output for the swapchain.
 * @param in Rasterized stage inputs containing UVs.
 * @param hdrTexture Primary offscreen RGBA16Float HDR color texture.
 * @param bloomTexture Accumulated compute bloom texture.
 * @param linearSampler Bilinear clamp sampler.
 * @param uniforms Composite parameters containing exposure and bloom intensity.
 * @return 32-bit presentation color.
 */
fragment float4 tonemap_fragment(TonemapVertexOutput in             [[stage_in]],
                                 texture2d<float>    hdrTexture     [[texture(0)]],
                                 texture2d<float>    bloomTexture   [[texture(1)]],
                                 sampler             linearSampler  [[sampler(0)]],
                                 constant TonemapUniforms& uniforms [[buffer(0)]]) {
  // Sample scene radiance and blurred bloom
  const float3 hdrColor   = hdrTexture.sample(linearSampler, in.texCoord).rgb;
  const float3 bloomColor = bloomTexture.sample(linearSampler, in.texCoord).rgb;
  // Composite scene color with scaled bloom optical glow
  const float3 combined = (hdrColor * uniforms.exposure) + (bloomColor * uniforms.bloomIntensity);
  // Compress dynamic range using ACES Filmic curve
  const float3 finalMapped = ACESFilm(combined);
  return float4(finalMapped, 1.0f);
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Source/EntryPoint.mm
/*!
 * @file EntryPoint.mm
 * @brief Global engine application startup and teardown entry point.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
#define UNIT_TESTING 1
#if UNIT_TESTING
#include "UnitTests/Phase1Test.hpp"
#endif
/*!
 * @brief Client-implemented application factory.
 * @return Dynamically created unique pointer to the Application subclass.
 */
extern std::unique_ptr<KanVerse::Application> KanVerse::CreateApplication();
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
  KanVerse::Log::Init();
  KV_INFO("KanVerse Engine Bootstrap Initiated.");
  // Creating core application and run the game loop
  {
    std::unique_ptr<KanVerse::Application> app = KanVerse::CreateApplication();
    if (!app) {
      KV_FATAL("Failed to instantiate KanVerse Application subclass!");
      return -1;
    }
    app->Run();
  }
  // At this point, app, its layers, buffers, and all destructors have completed
  auto stats = KanVerse::MemoryTracker::GetStats();
  if (stats.CurrentUsage > 0) {
    KV_WARN("Memory leak detected! {} bytes remaining in memory tracker.", stats.CurrentUsage);
  } else {
    KV_INFO("Teardown clean: 0 bytes leaked. Peak memory: {:.2f} MB",
                 static_cast<double>(stats.PeakUsage) / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
  }
  KV_INFO("KanVerse Engine Terminated Cleanly: All destructors executed.");
#if DEBUG
  KanVerse::Testing::RunPhase1Tests();
#endif
  KanVerse::Log::Shutdown();
  return 0;
}
// FILE: KanVerse_App/Source/KanVerseApp.mm
/*!
 * @file KanVerseApp.mm
 * @brief KanVerse client application bootstrap and entry point harness.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
/*!
 * @class KanVerseApp
 * @brief Concrete client specialization of the core engine application.
 */
class KanVerseKanVerseApp : public KanVerse::Application {
public:
  /*!
   * @brief Configures application properties and registers runtime layers.
   */
  KanVerseKanVerseApp()
  : Application(KanVerse::ApplicationProps{
    .Name = "KanVerse",
    .WindowWidth = 1280,
    .WindowHeight = 720,
    .VSync = true,
    .Maximized = true
  }) {
  }
  /*!
   * @brief Destructor for client application harness.
   */
  ~KanVerseKanVerseApp() override = default;
};
namespace KanVerse {
  /*!
   * @brief Factory implementation declared in Core/EntryPoint.hpp.
   * @return Unique pointer owning the initialized Application instance.
   */
  std::unique_ptr<Application> CreateApplication() {
    return std::make_unique<KanVerseKanVerseApp>();
  }
} // namespace KanVerse

// FILE: KanVerse_App/UnitTests/Phase1Test.hpp
/*!
 * @file Phase1Test.mm
 * @brief Comprehensive integration and unit test suite for KanVerse Phase 1 subsystems.
 * @copyright Copyright (c) 2026 KanVerse Contributors. All rights reserved.
 * @license MIT License
 */
// Required metal-cpp headers for full type definitions of MTL::Device and MTL::CommandQueue
#include <Foundation/Foundation.hpp>
#include <Metal/Metal.hpp>
namespace KanVerse::Testing {
  /*!
   * @brief Exercises all Phase 1 subsystems and validates correctness and memory safety.
   */
  void RunPhase1Tests() {
    KV_INFO("==================================================");
    KV_INFO("       STARTING KANVERSE PHASE 1 TEST SUITE       ");
    KV_INFO("==================================================");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 1: Diagnostics & Logging (All 5 Severities)
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    KV_TRACE("Phase1Test: Testing Trace level logging (Value: {:.4f})", 3.14159);
    KV_INFO("Phase1Test: Testing Info level logging.");
    KV_WARN("Phase1Test: Testing Warn level logging.");
    KV_ERROR("Phase1Test: Testing Error level logging.");
    // Note: We skip Fatal here as it triggers assertions/faults.
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 1: Diagnostics & Logging verified.");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 2: Branch-Predicted Assertions
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    int dummyValue = 42;
    KV_ASSERT(dummyValue == 42, "Dummy value must equal 42!");
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 2: Assertions verified.");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 3: Thread-Safe Memory Tracking & RAII Scoped Allocation Guards
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    {
      MemoryStats initialStats = MemoryTracker::GetStats();
      KV_INFO("Initial Heap Usage: {} bytes", initialStats.CurrentUsage);
      // Scope block to test RAII ScopedMemoryAllocation
      {
        ScopedMemoryAllocation testGuard(2048); // Simulate tracking 2 KB
        MemoryStats midStats = MemoryTracker::GetStats();
        KV_ASSERT(midStats.CurrentUsage >= 2048, "Memory tracker failed to record allocation!");
        KV_INFO("Inside Scope - Current Usage: {} bytes | Peak: {} bytes",
                     midStats.CurrentUsage, midStats.PeakUsage);
      } // Destructor automatically fires here, releasing 2048 bytes
      MemoryStats finalStats = MemoryTracker::GetStats();
      KV_INFO("Outside Scope - Current Usage: {} bytes", finalStats.CurrentUsage);
      KV_ASSERT(finalStats.CurrentUsage == initialStats.CurrentUsage,
                     "ScopedMemoryAllocation failed to unwind memory cleanly!");
    }
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 3: Memory Tracking & RAII Guards verified.");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 4: High-Resolution Monotonic Clock & TimeStep
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    {
      Clock testClock;
      testClock.Reset();
      // Sleep briefly to simulate frame delta
      std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(16));
      TimeStep dt = testClock.Tick();
      KV_INFO("Clock Tick Delta - Seconds: {:.4f}s | Milliseconds: {:.2f}ms",
                   dt.GetSeconds(), dt.GetMilliseconds());
      KV_ASSERT(dt.GetSeconds() > 0.0f, "TimeStep delta must be greater than zero!");
      float elapsed = testClock.GetElapsedSeconds();
      KV_INFO("Total Elapsed Wall Time: {:.4f}s", elapsed);
    }
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 4: Clock & TimeStep verified.");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 5: SIMD Linear Algebra & Matrix Transformations
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    {
      // Vector Construction
      Math::Vec3 v1 = Math::MakeVec3(1.0f, 2.0f, 3.0f);
      Math::Vec3 v2 = Math::MakeVec3(4.0f, 5.0f, 6.0f);
      Math::Vec3 vSum = v1 + v2;
      KV_ASSERT(vSum.x == 5.0f && vSum.y == 7.0f && vSum.z == 9.0f, "SIMD Vector addition incorrect!");
      // Matrix Identity & Multiplication
      Math::Mat4 identityMat = Math::Identity();
      Math::Mat4 translationMat = identityMat;
      translationMat.columns[3] = Math::MakeVec4(10.0f, 20.0f, 30.0f, 1.0f);
      Math::Mat4 multResult = Math::Multiply(identityMat, translationMat);
      KV_ASSERT(multResult.columns[3].x == 10.0f, "Matrix multiplication identity property failed!");
      // Projection Matrix Test (Metal [0, 1] Depth Space)
      float fov = 1.57079f; // ~90 degrees
      float aspect = 16.0f / 9.0f;
      Math::Mat4 projMat = Math::Perspective(fov, aspect, 0.1f, 100.0f);
      // Transform a point down the -Z axis to verify projection behavior
      Math::Vec4 testPoint = Math::MakeVec4(0.0f, 0.0f, -10.0f, 1.0f);
      [[maybe_unused]] Math::Vec4 projected = projMat * testPoint;
      KV_INFO("SIMD Perspective Projection matrix computed successfully.");
    }
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 5: SIMD Math & Matrices verified.");
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 6: Metal Hardware RenderContext Verification
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    {
      // Note: RenderContext requires an active Application instance or can be instantiated directly.
      // If testing outside Application::Run(), ensure MTLCreateSystemDefaultDevice() is valid.
      auto context = RenderContext::Create();
      if (context && context->GetDevice()) {
        KV_INFO("Metal Device Verified: %s", context->GetDevice()->name()->utf8String());
        KV_ASSERT(context->GetCommandQueue() != nullptr, "Primary command queue is null!");
        KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 6: Metal RenderContext verified.");
      } else {
        KV_WARN("[SKIP] Test 6: Metal device not available in headless environment.");
      }
    }
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Test 7: AudioSystem Subsystem Initialization
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    {
      AudioSystem::Init();
      // Test loading a non-existent sound to verify warning handling without crashing
      AudioSystem::LoadSound(SoundEffect::Jump, "NonExistentSoundCue.wav");
      AudioSystem::Shutdown();
    }
    KV_INFO("[PASS] Test 7: AudioSystem lifecycle verified.");
    KV_INFO("==================================================");
    KV_INFO("     ALL PHASE 1 TESTS COMPLETED SUCCESSFULLY     ");
    KV_INFO("==================================================");
  }
} // namespace KanVerse::Testing
